### v1.13.0 — Comic slab label rebuilt to match the real CGC labels (Nov 2, 2026)
Anthony asked whether the slabs' tops had been tightened (yes — v1.11.1 slimmed the band to ~15% of the slab) but said it still looked very generic next to the real thing, and authorized rebuilding the label "as best as possible" to match his actual CGC labels, modeled on his Aliens #1 and Batman #136 label photos. Answer recorded: the tightening had landed; the fidelity had not.
**What was wrong**: the SlabFront mock-up (comics.tsx) rendered the label as left-aligned stacked text — "CGC <label> / Cert N" header line, title, publisher line, then the variant and credits flattened into one comma-joined string each, "WHITE Pages", and the grade 9.8 plain on the RIGHT. None of a real label's anatomy was present.
**Rebuilt label (from his own label photos of all 8 slabs, already in the app as assets)**: top banner naming the grade type (CGC UNIVERSAL GRADE, white spaced caps on the dark blue band; CGC + script "Signature" + SERIES on the gold band); body in the real label colors (pale blue Universal, pale gold-cream Signature); the grade 9.8 huge in a white rounded box on the LEFT with "WHITE Pages" under it (Aliens #1's older label has no page line in the box — it prints WHITE Pages centered — reproduced as photographed); title and publisher/date centered and bold; signed line and edition line centered beneath; credits small at the bottom-left; cert number centered over a barcode whose bar pattern derives deterministically from that cert's own digits; the label's right-side note right-aligned beside the cert; a holographic CGC sticker block at the right end (rainbow foil Universal, silver foil Signature, blue with the scales mark on Aliens) plus Aliens' small gold Celebrity Authentics sticker (C12447); the faint oversized CGC watermark behind the middle, as printed. The band stays tight (~15% of the slab) and the holder gained its molded ridge under the label and the cover inset inside the case, like a real holder.
**Data**: every line is transcribed from his label photos — the Comic type gained labelTitle (labels drop the "(2021)"/"(2024)" year suffixes), labelPublisher ("D.C. Comics" on Batman's label), labelCredits (full names: "Donny Cates story / Iban Coello art / Gabriele Dell'Otto cover", etc. — the abbreviated log credits remain in use below the slab, unchanged), labelSigned, labelEdition, labelNote, labelLeftNote. Nothing invented: Wolverine #154 and Deadpool & Wolverine #3 have no edition line on their real labels and show none; Mandalorian #4 and Deadpool have no right-side note and show none. No CGC logo artwork was copied — banner, watermark, scales mark, barcode, and hologram are type and CSS shapes evoking the printed pieces. The "Label photo" flip view (his real label close-ups) is unchanged. Version 1.13.0 (minor: presentation rebuild of an existing surface).

### v1.14.0 — Profile header brand + modern controls; Settings "Your data & backups" with real collection exports (Nov 2, 2026)
**Header (Anthony, verbatim)**: "at the top of the home screen it says Vault X with basically like a black banner I want you to insert our icon in the middle there put Vault X right underneath it and I want that space to come up a little bit... move it up about 20%" + "Can you also change the way the back button and the three dots look? They look generic in my opinion. Make them look more modern." Identification: the only header in the app with BOTH a back button and a three-dots menu is the shared ProfileHeader (his own profile page, reached from Home via the Collector ID, and the friend profile — the identical component, per the follow-up's scope note). Applied there: the approved ring artwork (vault-x-ring-artwork.webp, already bundled) sits small (30px, round, accent glow) centered in the top band with "Vault X" in spaced caps directly underneath; the back and menu buttons keep their 44px touch areas and identical behavior/labels but now render sleek SVG glyphs (thin chevron; three clean dots) inside 36px frosted circular targets (white/7 blur, white/15 ring, soft blue-purple glow) instead of the generic bordered squares with text glyphs; the band's blank space is tightened (avatar row top margin mt-3 → mt-1.5 in the no-banner case) for roughly a fifth less vertical void. Nothing else on the profile changed.
**Settings — Your data & backups (Anthony)**: (1) he asked whether his cataloging work could be lost as he considers Vault X his main tracker, and directed baking the answer into Settings for other users too; (2) he directed building in export options "through XMLS, through CSV, through various different ways" (XMLS read as XLSX, the Excel format). New section directly after About this app, in the changelog's plain bullet voice, stating ONLY protections that exist as built: collection stored server-side in the app's cloud database tied to the user's account (not only on the phone — phone loss/switch loses nothing), Collector ID name/code also in the central registry, daily automatic price refresh with paid amounts kept as recorded, and the exports as the user's own keepable backup. No backup schedule, replication, or guarantee is claimed — none is implemented or verifiable.
**Exports**: new server action exportCollection returns every owned card by printing+finish (qty, unit market value, unit paid from the per-finish finance data) plus every sealed product at effective quantity (unit value/paid), uncapped and sorted; the client appends the 8 graded comics from its own catalog (qty 1, logged value, cert) and builds the file locally in client/src/exportCollection.ts — CSV (RFC-4180 escaping, CRLF, UTF-8 BOM), JSON (app/exportedAt/itemCount/items), and a real XLSX written by a dependency-free minimal OOXML stored-zip writer (shared strings, bold header style, column widths; verified pre-ship by generating a sample and reading it back with openpyxl — values, numbers, and cert strings intact). Files are delivered with the same share-sheet/blob-download helper pattern as the fixed Save-icon-artwork button (deliverFile), so repeated taps keep working on a phone; filenames are vault-x-collection-<date>.<ext>. Version 1.14.0 (minor: new Settings section + exports, header restyle).

### v1.15.0 — Supabase cloud backup sync + restore; Admin storage panel (Nov 2, 2026)
**Approval (Anthony, verbatim: "Yeah, let's do that.")**: make the existing Supabase project (iilsbousebrgkmsimucq — publishable key only, never the secret key; already embedded for the registry) the cloud backup for users' collections. **As built**: server/src/actions.ts writes the signed-in collector's FULL collection to the project's `collection_items` table (columns owner_collector_id, item_key, display_name, qty, kind, meta; unique on owner+item_key — upsert with the publishable key was probe-tested live: insert, merge-upsert, and owner-scoped delete all succeed; owner-only RLS stays as the project's model). Item keys: `card:{uid}:{finish}` per printing+finish, `sealed:{export name}`, `comic:{client catalog id}` (the 8 graded comics are mirrored server-side in GRADED_COMICS_CLOUD from client/src/comics.tsx COMICS — a static logged set). Display names mirror the existing server style ("Elsa - Snow Queen (Cold Foil)"). **Kept current**: full sync action `syncCollectionBackup` (upserts all local rows, deletes stale server rows, reads back to verify); incremental best-effort pushes inside the only two quantity editors — `updateOwnership` (per-card finish rows) and `setSealedQty` (per-item row) — so quantity +/-, scan adds, and any other edit through those actions syncs automatically; sync failures never block an edit (the next full sync heals). **Restore**: `restoreCollectionFromCloud` loads the backup back ONLY when the local catalog exists but owns zero items (fresh start) — it can never overwrite a live collection; the client calls it right after the first seed on an empty binder. Comics restore is a no-op by design (static in-app catalog). **Verified live before shipping**: profiles read-back shows MC-XFU8-Z5WD / ARUIKIS alongside MC-JARVIS and MC-TEST-DUMMY (Anthony's acceptance check 1); the initial full sync wrote 3,708 rows / 9,391 copies (3,685 card-finish rows / 9,368 copies, 15 sealed, 8 comics — matching the local export exactly), `getBackupStatus` reports inSync=true, and a sandbox setSealedQty bump to 2 appeared on the server without a manual sync, then was set back to 1 (acceptance check 2). **Settings wording** (the earlier honesty rule): "Your data & backups" now truthfully states the collection is backed up to the Vault X cloud server and restores on sign-in, and shows a LIVE backup status line from `getBackupStatus` plus a working "Back up now" button; the CSV/XLSX/JSON exports remain the user's own portable copy. **Admin storage panel (Anthony)**: `getAdminStats` system.storage — free-plan allotments (500 MB database, 1 GB file storage) as separate labeled bars in the owner-only Admin section. Database used = ESTIMATE (labeled as such in the UI): exact row counts via PostgREST count headers × average row bytes from a 100-row sample per table (profiles, collection_items, connections, trust_events) — Supabase exposes no exact byte size to the publishable key. File storage used = measured by listing storage buckets and summing object sizes; Vault X stores photos/files in the app's own blob storage, so the expected true figure is 0 buckets / 0 MB, labeled honestly in the UI when the listing cannot be read. Version 1.15.0 (minor: cloud backup feature + Admin storage panel).

### v1.16.0 — Example price-alert banner for users with no alerts (Nov 2, 2026)
**Direction (Anthony)**: when the app is shared, a user who has never created a price alert should see an EXAMPLE of the red Home banner so they know what it looks like before using one; tapping it teaches them, and they can dismiss it right then — gone until they create a real alert. **As built**: HomePanel shows the example only when getPriceAlerts returns ZERO alerts for the user AND the profile's dismissal flag is unset. The banner reuses the exact translucent-red oval styling of a real triggered alert, headed "Price alert" with a clearly visible EXAMPLE chip; its text says it is an example and invites a tap — deliberately NO item name or price (no fake trigger claim). Tapping opens a plain explanation sheet (what alerts do; set one from any item's Price Alert button at/below or at/above a price; when it triggers, the red banner appears at the top of Home and tapping it opens the item) with a prominent "Got it" button and an ✕ close — both dismiss immediately and permanently. **Persistence**: per user on the profile — migration 0013 adds collector_profile.example_alert_dismissed; dismissExampleAlert sets it; getPriceAlerts reports exampleDismissed alongside the alerts. Once a user has any real alert, the example never shows regardless of the flag. Anthony himself has a real triggered alert (Hyperia City Booster Box, at or below $200, currently $138.13), so his Home is unchanged — verified: his getPriceAlerts returns his alert and the example condition is false. **Test evidence**: in the sandbox only (durable data untouched — his alert row remains in the durable DB), the alert was deleted to simulate a new user: the audit render showed the EXAMPLE banner, the probe tapped it (sheet correct), tapped Got it (banner gone), and the dismiss action pair read back exampleDismissed=true.

### v1.17.0 — "Where it lives" storage locations on item details (Nov 2, 2026)
**Approval (Anthony)**: add the storage-location feature from the competitor research, placed where he asked — on the individual item's DETAIL view ("When you hone in on the card itself?"), modeled on the Judy Hopps - Lead Detective mockup he approved (Binder: Vault X XL — Binder 3 (Sets 13–14); Spot: Hyperia City page, pocket next to the foil; Deck copies in a deck box; Spare in Trade Box A). **As built**: new `item_locations` table (migration 0014) — target (card id / sealed name / comic id), loc_type (binder | deck_box | trade_box | bulk_box | other), place (binder/box name or free text), spot (page/pocket, row/section, or any precise detail), qty, created_at. Actions: listItemLocations / addItemLocation / setItemLocationQty / deleteItemLocation. Client: one shared component (client/src/whereItLives.tsx) rendered at the end of the detail body in all three detail sheets — CardSheet and SealedSheet (right after Price Alert) and ComicSheet (after the value block) — the rest of each detail (price block, charting, Best Offer, performance) untouched. The section lists each spot as "Type: place" + spot line + a copies stepper (36px +/−) + Remove, shows a placed-copies line against the owned total (including an honest "more than you own, worth a look" flag), and an add form with type chips whose place/spot labels and placeholder examples follow the approved mockup's pattern (placeholders are hints only — never stored). **Empty state**: items with no locations show an invitation to add one; NO location is seeded or invented for any item — the durable DB ships with zero location rows, and the two test rows created while verifying (Judy Hopps binder + Trade Box A spots) exist only in the sandbox copy. Anthony will review in-app and give his opinion; the section is deliberately self-contained so it is easy to restyle or move.

**Direction (Anthony, with a phone screenshot)**: he circled in red the top area of Home — the shell's "Vault X" title row plus the app's own centered ring + "VAULT X" label directly underneath it, with a tall blank band around them — as "the redundant area that needs to be cleaned up", confirmed with the main agent that the circled header was the target, and directed that the back button and the three dots "be refined to something more modern that's fabric looking" (read as a soft, fabric-like frosted finish; the main agent relayed it as a modern sleek look). **Identification**: inside the artifact, the duplicated branding lived in exactly two places — the Home header added in v1.17.1 (36px ring + spaced-caps "Vault X", duplicating the app name the shell already shows above the page) and the centered ring + "Vault X" in the shared ProfileHeader's top band (added v1.14.0). The back + three-dots pair exists only in ProfileHeader. **As built**: both branding marks are REMOVED — Home now opens straight into the Collector ID pill and price alerts, and a profile's top band is just its two controls floating over the content. The approved ring artwork is untouched where it belongs: Settings → About this app (with its Save button). The profile back and menu buttons keep their 44px touch areas, labels, haptics, and exact behavior (including the menu's Copy Collector ID / banner items), but the visible discs changed from solid frosted grey to a lighter modern fabric look: 40px translucent frosted-glass circles with a top-to-bottom light gradient, hairline white/10 ring, soft inner top highlight and drop shadow, backdrop blur, a gentle press scale, a finer 1.9-stroke chevron, and the menu dots running horizontally (matching the screenshot's •••) instead of vertically. ### v1.19.0 — Marketplace + Forums held from release; Trades fully operational (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Marketplace hold (Anthony, verbatim gist)**: "the marketplace is going to need more time to think about — I don't think that should be a release that the users should have available just yet. Let's keep the toggle there for it but let's say [coming soon]." Clarified the same evening: keep the Marketplace toggle/entry and the buy/sell-with-other-collectors area visible in place, NOT usable, delete the Elsa and Stitch artwork from it, and overlay "Coming soon" in yellow bubbly text running diagonally across it (down toward the right) until he reopens it. **As built**: one flag, MARKETPLACE_AVAILABLE = false in App.tsx. Home's doorway card stays in place with a yellow Coming soon pill and tapping it opens an explanation dialog instead of navigating. In Social, the Marketplace tab stays (with a yellow dot, like Forums) and renders MarketplaceHeldPanel: the area's heading, buy/sell description, section tabs and search bar all visible but disabled/inert, NO listings rendered — so the seeded Elsa - Snow Queen / Stitch - Rock Star listing artwork (the JARVIS_SEED_LISTINGS in server actions) never appears in the area — under a translucent veil with "Coming soon" in bright yellow bubbly lettering (.bubbly-soon in theme.css: rounded chunky face, dark outline, warm glow) rotated 18° diagonally down toward the right across the whole area. All marketplace data, listings, deals and server actions are untouched underneath; flipping the flag reopens it exactly as it was.
**Forums hold (Anthony, same evening — his spoken "form section" = Forums)**: the exact same treatment — everything in the Forums section stays in place under a translucent diagonal yellow "Coming soon" (.bubbly-soon, 18°), and the overlay takes the clicks so the section is not clickable, showing it still needs refinement. The Social intro copy now says Marketplace and Forums are both coming soon, held back for more refinement.
**Trades — OPERATIONAL at release (Anthony's correction)**: Trades must NOT get the Coming soon treatment — it has to be working at release. As reported by Anthony it was descriptive language only, nothing clickable. **As built (new tables trade_offers + trade_feedback, migration 0015)**: the full deal flow mirroring the Marketplace's verified pattern — Trade Match embedded in the Trades area (with Anthony's friend, the built-in test friend Jarvis), a Propose-a-trade composer (cards from each side, quantities, optional note), accept/decline for the partner, withdraw for the proposer, both-sides in-person confirmation, completion written to the Supabase trust_events ledger as trade_confirmed (+2 per side, retry-on-read like marketplace deals), and mandatory BLIND thumbs up/down feedback (tags + optional comment, revealed only when both sides are in, 7-day neutral close) that gates new proposals until given. Each side is validated against real stock server-side: Anthony's binder finishes (subject to his trade-availability setting) and Jarvis's three known cards (JARVIS_TRADE_STOCK, mirroring JARVIS_FRIEND_CARDS). Test-friend steps are labeled "(test)" buttons like marketplace deals, so the whole loop can be walked end to end. **Verified in the sandbox**: propose (Aurora - Holding Court Regular ×2 for Stitch - Rock Star ×1) → accept as test friend → both confirms → completed → both feedbacks → feedbackClosed with both ratings revealed and the gate clear.
**"I want this" flow (Anthony's vision)**: open a user, look through their collection, tap "I want this" on a card, choose how many copies, submit — that opens a NEW TRADE in the Trades area, finalized from there. **As built**: friend-collection tiles and Trade Match tiles (friend's side only) carry an "I want this" button; tapping it hands the card to the Trades area, which opens the composer with that card on the get side — the copy count is chosen with the composer's steppers (capped at how many the friend has) and the give side is picked before sending, so the submitted proposal is a complete new trade under Trades, finalized by the accept/confirm/feedback flow above.
**Trade availability setting (Anthony)**: a per-owner setting for how many copies of a card they are willing to trade or part with — tradeable quantity is SEPARATE from owned quantity. His personal setting: every card he owns MINUS ONE, one copy kept back for his master sets. **As built**: collector_profile.trade_mode (migration 0016) — keep_one (owned − 1; the default when never picked, matching his stated setting), all, or none — set from Settings → Trade availability and from the Trades area (one shared TradeAvailabilityControl), enforced server-side in trade validation (offering his only Cold Foil copy of Mickey Mouse - Best in Town under keep_one is rejected) and reflected in the composer ("you own N · M up for trade"). Trading setting only — the lifted minus-1 deck-build rule is untouched.
**Parked, NOT built (Anthony: "Put that all into the workshop")**: custom binders for others to look through, custom display decks, and copy allocation into BINDER/DECK sections (trade availability = what is left after allocations) — that goes into the trades research/brainstorm workshop with the examples from other apps he asked Jarvis to gather; it is deliberately not in this build. Version 1.19.0 (minor: Trades feature + holds).

### v1.20.0 — My Decks profile entry + decks page (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Profile entry point (Anthony, ~10:37 PM)**: on a user's profile, a pressable icon/button labeled "My Deck" / "My Current Decks" / "My Decks" (his wording options) that ports the viewer to a separate page showing off that user's decks nicely. **As built**: a MyDecksButton (stacked-cards icon, "My Decks" label, chevron) on Anthony's own profile page and on the Jarvis friend profile, alongside Trade Match and achievements; it opens a per-user DecksSheet ("My Decks" / "Jarvis's Decks").
**Honest empty state**: no user decks exist in any data source and the deck-building/display design itself stays in the workshop, so the page plainly says "No decks on display yet" — no invented decks. Its copy records the allocation tie-in: copies sitting in a deck are set aside in it and are not part of trade availability.
**Decks-page artwork (Anthony, ~10:39 PM, revised ~10:40 PM)**: he first directed pulling images of custom deck cases for the page (four custom deck-box photos, Etsy sellers, wooden/engraved cases, pulled and link-verified by the main agent), then REVISED it to ONE single really nice deck box image, with the user able to CHANGE THE COLOR of that deck box (a color choice per user/deck). The four referenced photos did not arrive with this build task (no files or URLs in the handoff), so the one image was sourced fresh via the image-search skill from the same source family (Etsy custom deck boxes): media URL https://i.etsystatic.com/21585322/r/il/923929/5544385807/il_1588xN.5544385807_acbz.jpg , source page https://www.etsy.com/listing/1609137633/lorcana-tcg-quad-deck-box-60-card-decks (Lorcana TCG quad deck box, laser-engraved wood). Preflighted (HEAD 200, image/jpeg), downloaded, visually verified: clean product shot of a real engraved Lorcana deck case, no seller watermark. Rejected candidates: a book-style wooden case (a large seller logo sign sits in the photo's background) and a blue leatherette case photo (shows another game's cards and dice). Stored owned at client/src/assets/deck-box-lorcana.jpg (downscaled to 1200px wide) and credited on the page with a link to the listing.
**Deck box color**: seven finishes (Natural wood, Walnut, Midnight, Royal blue, Crimson, Emerald, Violet) as stain/tint CSS filters over the single photo — the box never changes, only its finish. The picker lives on the owner's own decks page; the choice persists in localStorage ("vaultx-deck-box-color"), the app's existing device-preference pattern (same as save-mode/haptics). A friend's decks page shows the box in natural wood; per-deck colors arrive with the workshop deck design. Version 1.20.0 (minor: My Decks entry + decks page).
**Artwork correction (v1.20.1, same evening)**: the four pulled deck-case photos and their URLs arrived in a follow-up AFTER the 1.20.0 build, together with Anthony's revision: do NOT use four different photos — ONE single deck box image, specifically image 1 of the four, the high-resolution wooden customizable deck box (media URL https://i.etsystatic.com/55470037/r/il/4c12bb/7793938696/il_1080xN.7793938696_rhdz.jpg , source page https://www.etsy.com/listing/4462623898/wooden-deck-box-customizable-with , "Wooden Deck Box Customizable, magnetic top, laser engraved"), with the user able to change its color. The other three supplied photos (two from seller 56449292 via https://www.etsy.com/nz/r/similar/1613731695 and one via https://www.etsy.com/es/market/edh ) are NOT used as page artwork. As built in 1.20.1: image 1 (already downloaded and verified during candidate review: 1080x1232 JPEG, custom wooden deck box with a patterned magnetic top on a gaming table) replaced the interim Lorcana quad photo — stored owned at client/src/assets/deck-box-custom.jpg, the quad asset deleted, caption credit now links the supplied listing, no seller names/branding in the UI copy. The color-picker behavior is unchanged. Version 1.20.1 (patch: directed artwork swap).

### v1.20.3 — Home Marketplace "Coming soon" stamp moved onto "and comics" (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Correction (Anthony, verbatim)**: "No, no, I don't want it over marketplace itself. I want it over the words and comics so it says buy and sell cards sealed products and comics. I want the coming soon to be over that those two words." **As built**: on Home's Marketplace doorway card, the "Marketplace" label is plain again (no overlay, no dimming). The description line is now segmented so its final two words, "and comics", carry the stamp: those words sit dimmed underneath, and "Coming soon" sits directly over them in the same translucent toned-down yellow from v1.20.2, at the description's own text size (text-xs) so its band is exactly as tall as those two words. Everything else about the doorway (tap-to-explain dialog, hold flag, Social Marketplace/Forums holds) is unchanged. Version 1.20.3 (patch: stamp placement correction).

### v1.20.4 — Update-reload repair; Coming soon oval on the Marketplace line; "I want this" restyle (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Update bug (Anthony, verbatim)**: "I go into my settings, app updates, say I click check for updates. It says update is available. … Reload to update when I do that. It does not reload to 1.20 point 2." **Diagnosis**: detection was never the problem — Check for Update fetches the served page and bundle with `cache: "no-store"`, so it correctly saw the published build. The reload was a plain `window.location.reload()`, which the phone webview could answer from its HTTP cache, serving the OLD page and its old bundle — so he stayed on the old version no matter how many times he reloaded. **Fix (client/src/App.tsx, SettingsPanel)**: "Reload to update" now runs `reloadToUpdate` — it first re-fetches the page and every script the page references with `cache: "reload"` (forces a trip to the server AND replaces the cached copies with the fresh build), and only then calls `window.location.reload()`, so the reload can only serve the new build. Priming is best-effort (a failed prime still falls through to the reload), and the button shows "Reloading…" while it works. Counts as fixed only when Anthony reloads and actually sees the new version number in the app.
**Coming soon, final placement (Anthony, verbatim)**: "Now, so the coming soon is in the right area. But move it to be on the same line as marketplace." — plus the recorded refinement: an OVAL-shaped yellow pill/badge like the earlier one, translucent and toned down but definitely visible. **As built**: the pill (rounded-full, translucent yellow fill/border at 15%/35% alpha, 85%-alpha yellow text, small dark text-shadow) now sits inline next to the "Marketplace" label on the title line of Home's Marketplace doorway card, and the v1.20.3 overlay over "and comics" is gone — the description line is plain full-strength text again ("Buy and sell cards, sealed products, and comics with other collectors — not open yet"). The doorway's tap-to-explain dialog and the MARKETPLACE_AVAILABLE hold are unchanged, as are the Social Marketplace/Forums diagonal holds.
**"I want this" restyle (Anthony)**: the button on a friend's collection cards looked "too plain, too vanilla — white with the blue background"; he approved the richer direction (depth, soft glow, clearly pressable). **As built**: the label and behavior are unchanged; the flat accent fill is now a vertical gradient derived from the theme accent (lightened accent → accent → deepened accent, via color-mix so every theme tints correctly), with a top inner highlight, a darker inner bottom edge, a soft outer glow, a hairline white ring, slightly taller padding, a small two-way swap icon, and a gentle press scale. Version 1.20.4 (patch: update-flow repair + two directed restyles).

### v1.21.0 — Split "I want this" button with a cash-purchase third (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Direction (Anthony, verbatim)**: "I see the refinement in the I want this button. It looks better but let's do two things. Let's split that menu two thirds saying I want this in blue and then in green on the other third just have like an insignia, a fun insignia of cash. And that way you can automatically execute the cash trade right there and then." This replaces the plain v1.20.4 restyled single button wherever the friend-card button appears (friend profile collection tiles + the friend side of Trade Match). **Split button as built**: one pill with two independently tappable zones — left flex-2 blue gradient (the v1.20.4 restyle, swap icon + "I want this", behavior unchanged: opens the trade composer with the card on the get side), right flex-1 green gradient (same depth/inset/glow treatment, green family) carrying only a banknote CashIcon (client-drawn SVG: bill outline, center medallion, corner marks), split by a white/30 divider line, wrapper keeps the shared shadow + ring. When no cash handler is wired, the tile falls back to the v1.20.4 single button.
**Cash flow as built (mirrors the trade deal lifecycle exactly)**: new tables `cash_purchases` + `cash_feedback` (migration 0017). Tapping the cash third opens a CashPurchaseSheet showing the server-resolved price BEFORE anything is sent: `getCashQuote`/`resolveCashPrice` — the seller's live marketplace listing ask for the same printing+finish when one exists ("ask"), otherwise the card's current per-finish market value ("market"); quantity stepper defaults to 1, capped at the seller's known stock (JARVIS_TRADE_STOCK — the same stock trades validate against: Mickey ×1, Elsa ×1, Stitch ×2). `createCashPurchase` re-validates stock + price server-side and freezes the unit price and its source on the row. The request then follows the established buy flow in a new "Cash purchases" section inside the Trades area: seller accepts/declines (labeled "(test)" buttons for the test friend, like trade cards), buyer can withdraw while pending, both sides confirm once done in person, completion writes `marketplace_sale_confirmed` (+2 per side) to the Supabase trust ledger (retried on read, same as trades/deals), and mandatory BLIND thumbs feedback (cash_feedback, same tags, 7-day neutral close) finishes it; an unrated completed purchase gates new cash requests, enforced server-side and bannered in the section. **Honesty rules kept**: no payment moves in the app and the sheet + section say so plainly ("a request arranged between collectors — you settle payment directly"); a card with NO tracked price anywhere (Jarvis's Mickey Mouse - Brave Little Tailor — never priced in Anthony's data, deliberately never seeded a listing for the same reason) resolves to price null: the sheet says a cash request can't be priced for it and offers no Send, and createCashPurchase refuses server-side too — no number is ever invented. The held Marketplace is untouched (this is a separate path on the button, as directed). Seller scope is the test friend Jarvis — the only friend whose stock lives server-side, the same standing limitation as trades. Version 1.21.0 (minor: new feature — split button + cash purchase flow).
**Picker question — answered, rule NOT changed (Anthony: "still just report the current selection rule")**: the "You give" list in the trade composer is the first 30 rows of his own tradeable card stock in pure A–Z name order: getProfileItems (sort "name", filter "cards", group "all") returns owned printings split per finish, name-sorted (localeCompare, stable), capped at 240; the composer then drops rows whose per-finish tradeable count under his "Keep one of each" setting is zero and slices the first 30. No trade-match, value, or recency logic. His follow-up ("if they were using value as a metric, that would be awesome") is noted as a possible future direction only — not implemented in this build.

### v1.22.0 — Trade/cash carts + checkout with Fill my order (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Direction (Anthony, verbatim)**: "Build it out Jarvis. Build it out." — a major design expansion SUPERSEDING v1.21.0's instant-execute cash sheet. **Visibility rule**: the split button renders on a friend's card ONLY when the owner has copies available to part with (tile `availableQty` = the friend item's copies; zero → the button does not exist at all, never greyed). **Carts, not instant execution**: blue side adds to a trade cart, green side adds to a cash cart (tap again for another copy, capped at the owner's available copies); carts live in SocialPanel state (per card+finish lines with maxQty), tiles show "In trade cart ×N · In cash cart ×N", and Jarvis's profile shows a cart bar with distinct-card counts and a Go to checkout button into the Trades area. **Trade checkout**: the trade cart seeds the trade composer's get side on entering Trades (composer auto-opens); the new **Fill my order** button (server action `fillTradeOrder`) builds the give side from Anthony's tradeable cards (his availability setting) that Jarvis does NOT own (Trade Match direction), greedy by value descending, adding copies only while the give total stays ≤ the get total — equal or less by construction, so the owner is never shorted; both totals are shown and the filled side lands in the normal give picks, fully amendable (steppers, remove, search-add) before Send (existing proposeTrade; sending clears the cart). **Cash checkout**: new `CashCheckoutSection` in Trades above Cash purchases — per-line steppers/remove, live server pricing via `priceCashCart` (same resolveCashPrice: listing ask else per-finish market value), total labeled a pre-estimate at current market value; a line with no tracked price blocks the send until removed (Mickey case, never an invented number). `createCashOrder` validates ALL lines first (stock, price, feedback gate) and fails the whole order on the first bad line; on success each line becomes its own `cash_purchases` row (frozen price/source) so the owner accepts/declines card by card, then the verified v1.21.0 lifecycle runs per card (both confirm → trust ledger → blind feedback). The v1.21.0 CashPurchaseSheet UI was removed as superseded (getCashQuote/createCashPurchase actions remain valid API). No payment in the app; checkout copy says so. Seller scope remains the test friend Jarvis, same standing limitation as trades. Version 1.22.0 (minor: new feature — carts + checkout).

### v1.23.0 — Sealed header deleted; Great Hunny Rescue selectable (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Sealed header**: the shared Collection summary / pockets-filled block no longer renders on the Sealed view (App.tsx gate narrowed from `binder || sealed` to `binder` only — removed from the document, not CSS-hidden; Binder unchanged, per Anthony: "that whole section needs to be deleted" for Sealed). **Quest 3 question answered from source**: the sealed catalog held NO Illumineer's Quest boxes (Q1–Q3 exist only as binder card sections); cataloged sealed items have always been addable via the shelf steppers (setSealedQty), but a product NOT in the catalog had no in-app path — new sealed items previously arrived only via a Shiny export update. **Great Hunny Rescue**: added natively via the established hand-sourced route — `extraSealedItems` in exportFinance.ts with qty **0** (selectable, deliberately NOT added to Anthony's owned count; he adds his sealed copy himself, second box being opened), setName "Attack of the Vine!", paid 0 (not recorded, Fabled-pack precedent). **Pricing — the app's normal sealed source, TCGplayer**: product ID **701135**; market price **$34.24** observed Nov 2, 2026 via TCGplayer's catalog data (TCGCSV mirror, snapshot 2026-11-02T20:06Z; research dir ~/workspace/research_notes/great-hunny-rescue-tcgplayer-20261103-0517). The brief's ~$34.49 was NOT used — the source's actual figure was. One sealedHistory point (2026-11-02, "Nov 2", 34.24, source "tcgplayer") starts its value chart, Japanese-pack pattern; detail gets the standing chart + Best Offer (logo link https://www.tcgplayer.com/product/701135, the app's standard ID-form URL). **Box art**: the real official Ravensburger box render (Asmodee CA product shot, SKU RAV11090038, 1200×1200, found via image search; source page https://shop.asmodee.ca/autres-10), white background flood-cut to transparency, stored as client/src/assets/sealed/item-great-hunny-rescue.webp and rendered as a clean cut-out inside the CSS acrylic case (Japanese-pack pattern; NOT in SEALED_IN_CASE). getSealed items gained a `unitValue` field so qty-0 catalog tiles show the real per-unit market value ("$34.24 Value each · not in your vault yet") instead of a misleading $0.00 total; the Paid line is replaced by an "In the catalog" note at qty 0. Shelf intro copy updated to name both catalog additions and their pricing sources. Version 1.23.0 (minor: new selectable catalog item).
**Remaining Shiny dependence (for the announced refinement pass, per Anthony's "We can't just depend on Shiny" direction)**: (1) CARDS — the card catalog itself is Ravensburger data and ownership counts were seeded once from the Nov 1 Shiny export, but ownership is fully editable natively (plus/minus, Save, scan-a-card) and prices refresh daily from TCGplayer, so cards no longer NEED Shiny day-to-day; the export remains only the historical source of the starting counts. (2) SEALED — the shelf's base catalog and its per-item values/paid still come from the Nov 1 export for the original 14 items; sealed values move only on a new export (no daily sealed re-pricing); new sealed products have no in-app "add new product" flow — they join the catalog only when someone adds them in code (the Japanese pack and now Great Hunny Rescue are the two native exceptions). Quantities on cataloged items are natively editable. (3) COMICS — the graded-comics catalog is a fixed client-side list of Anthony's 8 slabs from his own records; there is no in-app add-a-comic flow at all yet. The Shiny import path itself was NOT removed — his existing collection data stays exactly as imported.

### v1.24.0 — Messaging, hybrid availability, checkout page, update indicator + test, scan search (Nov 2, 2026, late)
**Split-button sizing (measured, probe bboxes)**: original strip ≈40px tall → squeezed 25px (Anthony: "a lot thinner", "squeeze it in") → final **29px** after his exact directive "whatever that figure is, add 15%" (25 × 1.15 = 28.75; implemented as py-1.5 on both zones + the single-button fallback; verified 29px on the published build's probe). Label no longer wraps (whitespace-nowrap, 11px, smaller swap icon) so the slim strip stays one line in the narrow friend-grid tiles. **Docked checkout bar**: bright-green long bar fixed directly above the bottom menu (the "Send purchase order" bar's shape, brighter green per his direction), rendered only while browsing a friend's collection with a non-empty cart — appears on the first add, updates counts/cash total, never forces a tap. **Checkout page**: the bar's Checkout now opens a dedicated full-screen Checkout page (TradesPanel content on its own screen, "Back to browsing" returns) and the green bar is gone while it is open; CashCheckoutSection moved to the top of the Trades flow (right after Trade availability) so checkout no longer sits at the bottom. **Cash pricing**: priced at current-day TCGplayer value (the daily-repriced per-finish market figure) × qty; the listing-ask override was removed from resolveCashPrice. Buyer may change the total (offer field; $0 gifts allowed — migration-free, zod floor lowered to min 0); offered lines scale to the total with the last line absorbing the remainder at 3-decimal precision so offered totals sum exactly (verified: Elsa×1 + Stitch×2 at a $2.00 offer stored $1.29 + $0.71 = $2.00). Migration 0020 adds suggested_unit_price; the receiver's card is labeled "Proposed cash trade" and shows offered vs the system-computed "TCG suggested market price". The $0 send-button bug from his live test: sandbox pricing was correct ($2.00/$0.55); the button now shows "Pricing…" or a retry state instead of ever rendering $0 from a pending/failed quote. **Trade Availability (hybrid, migration 0018, column trade_availability)**: "master" and "decks" hold-backs are stackable checkboxes; "all"/"none" are exclusive; tradeable = owned − (master ? 1 : 0) − deck-box copies (item_locations deck_box rows, counted against Regular first). Server computes tradeableQty for profile items; validateTradeItems/fillTradeOrder/buildProfileItems all use it. Default remains "master". **Trade-only messaging (migration 0019, deal_messages)**: a thread exists ONLY inside a trade or cash purchase; text only (no attach UI anywhere); links detected client- and server-side and blocked with a plain note; no age gate (declined); party-only enforced server-side in resolveDealForThread (a message is only ever returned to one of the deal's two parties); thread closes on completion/decline/cancel, history stays on the deal record. Storage is the app's own server DB behind its actions — the same pattern as trades/offers/feedback — NOT a new Supabase table (this build environment has no Supabase DDL path; disclosed in the build report). Footnote at the very bottom of every thread, footnote-sized, in red, exact wording: "* Vault X doesn't send push notifications — you'll only see new messages when you open the app." **Social order**: Friends, Trades, Marketplace, Forums. **Sharing (migration 0022, share_collection, default 1)**: the per-friend sharing switch moved out of Social into Settings > Sharing, ON by default, server-persisted via setShareCollection; the Jarvis-profile sharing preview block was removed. **Update indicator + one-time test**: the app auto-checks on open (fetchLatestBuild); a yellow "Update available" pill sits on Home to the LEFT of his username; updating stays a tap, never automatic. The one-time TEST (flag update_test_seen, migration 0021) opens a dress-rehearsal walkthrough in Settings > App updates (real changelog + Finish button); the flag is consumed ONLY on finishing. His first test burned on an earlier click-to-dismiss build, so this publish re-arms it via the post-submit action resetUpdateTest. **Scan-a-card fix (after his live test — slow + wrong "Merlin" match)**: the scan result screen now carries a "Wrong card — or no match?" catalog search (name, or name + number per the standing rule) whose results render as the same add-rows a correct scan uses (searchCards extended additively with rarity/finishes/marketValue/suggestedFinish); the matcher was tightened so a read collector number is a HARD filter (only that exact printing can match; no printing with that number → unmatched, where the search takes over), and the vision prompt now demands exact title/number reads with null instead of a guess. Identification speed is one vision round-trip; the catalog search path is instant. Version 1.24.0 (minor: messaging + features).

### v1.25.0 — Availability placement, How-trading-works toggle, invoice checkout, $0-send + Mickey price fix, per-friend sharing, pockets header deleted everywhere, refresh-in-place, standalone update view, all Quest boxes, change requests, ARuikis (Nov 3, 2026)
**Trade Availability placement**: the section was removed from TradesPanel entirely (its last non-Settings home was inside TradesPanel, which the checkout page also embeds — Anthony's page stack "Checkout → Trades / Trade offers → Your trade availability → Cash checkout" was that one component rendering in both places). It now renders ONLY in Settings; the setting still governs button visibility and cart caps behind the scenes. The trade composer's note now points to Settings. **How trading works toggle (migration 0023, collector_profile.how_trades_work_hidden)**: Hide collapses the explainer to its slim title row with a Show control; the choice persists per user via the new setHowTradesWorkHidden action; getTrades returns the flag. **Invoice checkout**: the cash checkout is a clean invoice — one row per card (name + printed number, right-aligned line total, finish · price-each line, qty stepper, Remove), then a divider-separated totals block (TCG suggested total right-aligned; Your offer total with a right-aligned input). The trade composer gained the same invoice-style totals block (You give / You get totals and the difference line); TradePick now carries unitValue, and fill-produced picks use the fill's server-computed totals only while the picks' signature matches the fill (client totals take over the moment he amends). Send purchase order is full-width in the brighter green gradient (#4ade80 → #22c55e), matching the docked bar. **$0 send + Mickey price — root cause found**: Anthony's $0 failures were (a) a pre-$0 build on his phone and (b) Mickey Mouse - Brave Little Tailor (cardId 115, uid "1:115") carrying NO price anywhere (catalog finance empty, marketValue null), which made priceCashCart flag the line unpriced and block the send. Real values sourced Nov 3, 2026 from Lorcast (the app's own pricing source): Mickey Regular **$18.43**, foil **$83.31** (TCGplayer product 508785). Seeded into the sandbox via saveRepricing and verified end-to-end: cart quote $18.43 for Mickey, three-card total **$21.53** (Mickey 18.43 + Elsa - Snow Queen Cold Foil 2.00 + Stitch - Rock Star 0.55 × 2), and a $0.00 createCashOrder succeeded. The durable database picks the price up through the app's own daily repricing (refreshFinance prices every catalog uid that has a TCGplayer id, owned or not); a manual run of the daily job was queued at publish. Jarvis's profile total now reads $21.53. **Per-friend sharing (migration 0024, friend_sharing)**: Settings > Sharing now lists one switch per connected friend (registry members; built-in Jarvis always present), every switch ON by default (no row = on); getSharingSettings/setFriendSharing are the actions. This supersedes the v1.24.0 single global switch UI (the global column/action remain in the API but nothing renders them). **Pockets-filled header — TOTAL deletion**: the Collection summary section's CONTENT was deleted from the binder view (the section shell remains only as the loading/error surface), the pockets clause came out of every individual set page's sub-line, and the Missing-cards sheet dropped its pockets clause (plain "N missing" kept). The set tiles' own compact collected-progress readout and bars stay — that is the tiles' designed element, not the deleted header. **Refresh-in-place**: SocialPanel's trade cart, cash cart, and checkout-page state now persist in sessionStorage (vaultx-social-nav-v1, same pattern as the main view), so a pull-to-refresh on the checkout page restores the checkout page with the cart intact instead of landing back on the friend's profile with an empty cart. **Standalone update view**: tapping either Home update pill (real or the one-time TEST) now opens a dedicated full-screen update view — changelog plus "Update" and "Don't update at this time" — instead of routing to Settings. Backing out leaves the choice unmade; the TEST is consumed (via dismissUpdateTest) only when he picks either choice and finishes the flow; the real Update button runs the same cache-priming reload as Settings. The walkthrough was removed from Settings (Settings keeps its manual check/reload). The test re-arms on this publish via the post-submit resetUpdateTest so he can retest the corrected routing. **Quest boxes completed**: Illumineer's Quest: Deep Trouble (TCGplayer 543862, market **$50.28**) and Illumineer's Quest: Palace Heist (623065, **$26.31**) added to extraSealedItems at qty 0 / paid 0 — prices from TCGplayer's catalog data (TCGCSV snapshot 2026-11-02T20:06Z), setName Ursula's Return / Reign of Jafar respectively, one tcgplayer-sourced history point each. Box art flood-cut from the official quest tile photos (Q1 white backdrop, Q2 black backdrop) into item-deep-trouble.webp / item-palace-heist.webp, registered in SEALED_PHOTOS (acrylic-case presentation). Every Quest set page in the binder (quest:Q1/Q2/Q3) now shows a sealed-box banner (QUEST_SEALED_BOX map) that opens the sealed item detail; Anthony's sealed totals unchanged (15 items, $312.58 — all three Quest boxes at ×0). The way any user adds a sealed product: Sealed shelf → find the catalog item → tap + (setSealedQty); products not in the catalog still have no in-app add flow (see the Shiny-dependence list in the v1.23.0 entry — unchanged by this release except that all three Quest boxes are now cataloged natively). **Change requests (migration 0025, change_requests)**: Settings gains "Send a change request" directly under App updates — type picker (Change request / Bug / Idea), text message (1–1000 chars), submitted via submitChangeRequest and stamped server-side with the sender's username, Collector ID, and date. Anthony reviews them in the owner-only Admin section (getChangeRequests gates on OWNER_COLLECTOR_CODE = MC-XFU8-Z5WD; non-owners get an empty, hidden inbox). **Username restyle (migration 0026)**: Anthony's display username is now **ARuikis** (was ARUIKIS) — the guarded migration renames only his exact row (collector code MC-XFU8-Z5WD + old username), and the profile-creation default matches; the Supabase registry copy updates on his next registry sync (the sync upserts the profile username). Version 1.25.0 (minor: features).

### v1.26.0 — Update view final form, refresh-in-place everywhere, checkout slimming + flow separation, unpriced never blocks, Home value line, My Decks back fix + icon, Settings rearrangement + jump icons, Sealed Owned/All + sorts + type filter, all 14 Illumineer's Troves (Nov 3, 2026)
**Unpriced-blocker diagnosis (Anthony's question — YES)**: the yellow "No prices tracked" notice WAS the blocker on his build, in two places — the checkout Send button was disabled while any line was unpriced, and createCashOrder returned a hard fail telling him to take the item out and send the rest. Root cause stayed the same: Jarvis's Mickey carried no price row. Both gates are removed: createCashOrder no longer fails on unpriced lines (they ride along at offered 0 and are excluded from the suggested total; a buyer-set offer total is honored even when the suggested total is 0), the Send button is never disabled by an unpriced line, and the notice is information-only wording. Per-line Remove at checkout already existed; remove-and-send-the-rest works there now. Receiver side: CashPurchaseCard shows "No TCG price is tracked for this card yet — the offer stands on its own" when suggestedTotal is null. **Update view final form**: heading is "App updates", the view is ONLY that section alone, and the bottom choices are "Update now" / "Update at a later time" — the separate "Finish the test" step is gone; either choice completes the walkthrough (Update now auto-completes after the rehearsal apply message), Back leaves the test armed. Re-armed on this publish via post-submit resetUpdateTest. **Refresh-in-place — full map**: navigation state lives in history entries + sessionStorage. Already restoring before this release: main top-level views (sessionStorage mycollectibles-view-v1), Social area + Jarvis friend profile (history entry read on mount), carts + checkout page (sessionStorage vaultx-social-nav-v1, v1.25.0). The relocation cause: overlay/section states (binder group, card sheet, sealed sheet, missing sheet, Collector ID/profile) were plain React state initialized empty, and nav normalization treated their history entries as fresh opens. Fixed by seeding those states from the surviving window.history.state entry on mount (groupKey/showPicker/binderGroupPushedRef from mcBinder, selectedId/sheetPushedRef from lorcanaCard, sealedSheetName, missingSheetKey, CollectorIdCard expanded/profileOpen) and widening nav-init's intact-stack check to those keys; a restored group key that no longer exists falls back to the set picker. In-page filter/search text is component state and still resets on refresh (acceptable scope, stated in the report). **Checkout slimming**: the checkout page is the heading window + the checkout itself — TradesPanel gained a `checkout` variant that drops its header/intro, the How-trading-works window, Trade Match, the composer closed-state button, and ALL offer/purchase lists; CashCheckoutSection renders bare (no duplicate "Cash checkout" heading) so the page reads Checkout → invoice. The heading + tagline now sit in a Hide/Show window in the How-trading-works panel style (heading-window hide is session-local state). **Flow separation**: in checkout mode the trade composer renders only when the trade cart is non-empty — a cash-only checkout shows the cash invoice alone (Anthony: "There was no trade to propose. The trade is cash."). The duplicate Trades intro paragraph was also removed (How trading works is the single explanation). **Home**: Collection value moved onto the Your Collections heading line, offset right (label + grand total only); the per-collection breakdown lines are deleted from Home. The Collection Distribution card stays. **My Decks**: the decks sheet (and the achievements sheet, same family) now uses a shared useHistorySheet hook — opening pushes its own history entry, so Back from My Decks returns to the profile he came from (previously decksOpen was plain state and Back popped the profile's own entry, landing him on Collector ID). The profile entry shrank to a 56px circular deck-box-photo icon with no visible text (aria-label kept), under the header/Trust Score area on both profiles. **Settings**: sections reordered most-used first (App updates, Send a change request, Theme, Sharing, Save collection count, Haptics, Trade availability, Your data and backups, About, Admin) with ids on each section and a centered row of circular quick-jump icon buttons under the heading (scrollIntoView smooth); the Admin chip renders only for the owner (shares the adminStats query). Admin storage bars (Supabase free-tier allotments with usage bars) were verified already present from v1.15.0 — no change needed. **Sealed shelf**: Owned / All available toggle (default Owned; default unchanged for his shelf — 16 owned items), type filter (Boxes/Packs/Quests/Troves by name classification), sorts (catalog, value high/low by unitValue, name, set, date added newest/oldest). Date-added sorting rides on migration 0027 (sealed_qty.updated_at, stamped by setSealedQty): honestly, dates exist only for quantities set in-app; undated items keep catalog order at the end and the UI says so when a date sort is active. In All available, qty-0 items render ghostly (.ghost-img on the tile photo, the missing-card treatment). The deck-box photo still fronts the Decks page itself.

Owner envelope: `OwnerEnvelope` on Home, left of the Collector ID pill, renders only when `getChangeRequests` returns rows (owner-gated server-side, so it can only ever be Anthony's), red envelope + count badge, tap → Settings scrolled to the Admin inbox (`#settings-change-requests`).

The Admin inbox lists messages (sender/type/date/preview, unread bold + red dot) and opens one like an email (From/Date/Type/body, Back to inbox); the Home envelope badge counts unread only.
- **Envelope end-to-end + Search keyboard + hideable intros (v1.29.0, Nov 3, ~1:34 AM steers)**: (1) The envelope → inbox path is exercised end to end: TEST envelope on Home (left of the Collector ID pill) taps through the existing `onOpenChangeRequests` redirect (Settings scrolled to `#settings-change-requests`) into the email-style `ChangeRequestsInbox`; a `resetEnvelopeTest` action (mirroring `resetUpdateTest`) re-arms the test flag — used to restore the sandbox flag after verification consumed it, so the audit capture shows the envelope. (3) Hideable intro windows: migration 0030 adds `sealed_intro_hidden` / `social_intro_hidden`; `getSectionIntros` / `setSectionIntroHidden` persist the choices; shared `useSectionIntro` + `HideableIntroWindow` (bordered window, Hide → title row + Show) wrap the Sealed intro (title "Sealed" + the "kept mint in acrylic" text; shelf controls/items untouched) and the Social intro (title "Social" + the connections text; areas untouched). Fix mirrors the Update-available test pattern: migration 0029 adds `collector_profile.envelope_test_seen`; `getChangeRequests` also returns `envelopeTestSeen`; until the test is completed, `OwnerEnvelope` shows a TEST-tagged red envelope (badge 1) when no real unread requests exist — real unread always takes its place — and tapping it uses the same redirect to Settings → `#settings-change-requests`.
