## v1.28.0 (Nov 3, 2026) — Mickey price root-caused and fixed in code, checkout cancel actions

- **Mickey Mouse - Brave Little Tailor pricing — the real root cause**: v1.25.0's fix relied on the agent-driven daily repricing (refreshFinance spawns a task; saveRepricing writes DB prices) landing uid "1:115" in the durable database. The cron job reported success at spawn time, but no price for Mickey ever landed durably, and every pricing surface that reads ONLY the binder DB row (resolveCashPrice for the cash checkout quote, getCard's marketValue for the friend profile, fillTradeOrder's valueOf for trade totals) therefore showed no price — Anthony's live retest (Nov 3, ~1:07 AM) still showed nothing. Meanwhile the static Lorcast snapshot (catalogPrices.ts) carried the card all along. **Fix**: new shared server helper `cardFinishMarketValue(row, finish)` implementing the same fallback chain pocketPrice already used (tracked per-finish DB price → same-foil DB price → Lorcast snapshot by TCGplayer id → row marketValue → null), now used by resolveCashPrice, fillTradeOrder's valueOf, and getCard's marketValue fallback. The fix is in code, so it holds in every environment without depending on the repricing pipeline; when the daily repricing does land a fresher DB price it supersedes the snapshot as before. **Snapshot refresh**: the 508785 (Mickey) entry in catalogPrices.ts was updated from the Oct 26 snapshot values (19.94 / 82.94) to the Nov 3, 2026 Lorcast values sourced in the v1.25.0 session (Regular **18.43**, foil **83.31**) — the freshest sourced figure and the one already reported to Anthony. Expected Jarvis test-cart quote: Mickey 18.43 + Elsa Cold Foil 2.00 (DB) + Stitch 0.55 × 2 (DB) = **$21.53**.
- **Checkout cancel actions (Nov 3, ~1:08 AM steer)**: the cash checkout page gains a red **Cancel this purchase** button beside Send purchase order — the red counterpart of the bright green (same pill shape, weight, ring, and shadow family; gradient #f87171 → #ef4444 vs the green #4ade80 → #22c55e). Tapping it exits the checkout page; nothing is sent, no order is created, and the cart is left as it was. The trade composer's cancel is now **Cancel this trade** in the same red; on the checkout page it likewise exits the page, and in the Trades area it closes the composer as before. Wiring: TradesPanel gained `onCancelCheckout`, passed from SocialPanel's checkout page (`setCheckoutPage(false)`); CashCheckoutSection renders the cancel only when an onCancel handler is wired (checkout page only). Version 1.28.0 (minor: features).
- **Ticket system (v1.30.0, Nov 3, ~1:39 AM steer — SUPERSEDES the email model)**: Anthony scratched the email/Outlook inbox treatment ("SCRATCH the email/Outlook-style inbox treatment") for a work-style TICKET system. Migration 0031 adds `change_requests.status` (open/in_progress/completed, default open) + `assignee` (nullable display name). `submitChangeRequest` opens a ticket and returns its id; new actions `getMyTickets` (submitter follows their own tickets: status + assignee + date), `setTicketStatus` and `setTicketAssignee` (both owner-gated); `getChangeRequests` rows carry status + assignee; `getAdminStats`' pending count is now OPEN tickets. `markChangeRequestRead`/`read_at` remain in the schema but are unused by the UI. Client: `TicketsInbox` (list with status chips/#id/kind/assignee, detail with Set status + Assign controls) renders in the Settings Admin section AND in a dedicated standalone tickets window at the App root (own history entry, Back closes) — the Home envelope opens ONLY that window, never Settings (Anthony's correction, twice). The one-time envelope TEST now shows as a test ticket in that list; finishing it retires the test. Envelope restyle per his refinement: plain red envelope icon only (no circle/fill), tight against the Collector ID pill, count badge on the LEFT, count = open tickets (test = 1). The submit form shows "Your tickets" (own tickets with status/assignee). Also in v1.30.0: checkout cancels FULLY clear their carts (his Stitch bug report — the v1.28.0 cancel only navigated); the Settings shield jump icon flicker ROOT-CAUSED (async-gated render + useRef latch resetting on remount; the chip now renders unconditionally — the Admin section stays owner-gated server-side); Portfolio title in a bordered window; profile name merged onto the deck-box icon's line (icon position unchanged, border toned to border-2 accent/30). Version 1.30.0 (minor: features + fixes).
- **Order messages in checkout (v1.31.0, Nov 3, ~1:51 AM steers)**: the cash checkout gains a "Message with this order" compose BELOW the Send/Cancel buttons. `createCashOrder` accepts optional `message` (max 500, no links — DEAL_LINK_PATTERN, same rule as deal messages); on success it seeds the text as the opening `deal_messages` row (kind "cash") of EVERY purchase the order creates, so the message travels with the order and the thread continues inside each purchase (both directions via the existing DealMessages + sendDealMessage; seller answers as before, incl. the test-side buttons; the red no-push disclaimer already closes every thread and now also sits under the checkout compose). `cashPurchaseView` gains `orderMessage` (earliest buyer message per purchase, computed in getCashPurchases from deal_messages) and the purchase card shows it as a labeled quote under the invoice lines — invoice + wants + message in one view. Trade parity: the composer note moved BELOW Send/Cancel, relabeled "Message with this trade" (textarea, 280 max), link-guarded client+server, and `proposeTrade` seeds it as the trade thread's opening message (the note still shows quoted on the trade card). Also in v1.31.0: Search overlay panel re-anchored low (flex justify-end, pb-[24vh], results max-h 42vh) per his thumb-reach direction, keyboard still never auto-opens; friend-profile name strip (name + Built-in friend chip + BadgeRow) moved out of ProfileHeader (new `nameStripMoved` mode, header name cell empty, deck icon unmoved) to a strip at the top of JarvisFriendProfile starting at the Trades tab's left edge (ml-[25%]); Portfolio summary block (As-of line, hero total, paid/gain/items/averages, foil paragraph) tidied into one bordered window with aligned dl rows — tabs/charts below unchanged. Version 1.31.0 (minor: features).
- **Home bottom note window (v1.31.0 addendum, Nov 3, ~1:52 AM steer)**: the two-pockets explainer at the foot of the page moves into a bordered `HideableIntroWindow` ("Two pockets for every card number", wording unchanged), persisted by migration 0032 (`home_intro_hidden`) through the same getSectionIntros/setSectionIntroHidden pair with section "home".

## v1.32.0 — Zelle, order-mail indicators, invoice cleanup, search keyboard sync (Nov 3, 2026)
- **Zelle handle**: user-entered in Settings > Payment info, stored on `collector_profile.zelle_handle` (migration 0033) via getPaymentInfo/setZelleHandle — never hard-coded, never on the public profile. `cashPurchaseView.sellerZelleHandle` resolves it only when the seller is this device's collector; the purchase order renders the "Pay the seller" line (Zelle mark + handle + Copy) to the buyer inside the order only. Vault X processes no payments. The split button's green segment pairs the cash icon with a drawn Zelle mark (purple rounded square, white Z — a glyph drawn in code, not a copied logo asset).
- **Order-mail unread**: `deal_thread_reads` (migration 0034) holds per-viewer thread read markers; `getOrderMailStatus` counts other-party messages newer than the marker across the viewer's trades and cash purchases; `markDealThreadRead` upserts on thread open (DealMessages marks on open and as messages land). Indicators: Home mail icon beside the Collector ID pill (accent, count badge on the left; red envelope stays tickets-only), badge on the Social Trades entry, and an "N new" chip on each order's Messages toggle.
- **Purchase order invoice**: the cash purchase card is restructured to the checkout's invoice standard — header (counterparty, date, status chip), aligned line item, totals block (Offer total / TCG suggested), Zelle line, order message, then the thread in its own section. `cashPurchaseView` gained buyerUsername + iAmSeller for the header framing.
- **Profile spacing**: the stretch between the Social areas bar and the Collection/Trade Match tabs on friend profiles is condensed (margins tightened through the strip, header rows, bio/joined, trust row, explainer, tab bar) — nothing removed or moved.
- **Search keyboard lag — diagnosed cause**: the overlay's full-screen backdrop-blur re-rasterized the whole app on every keyboard-animation frame, and the panel's position was layout-driven (full-height flex + vh padding), so it trailed the keyboard. Fix: while the search field is focused, the overlay drops the backdrop blur and pins its height to `visualViewport.height` (updated on visualViewport resize), so the bottom-anchored panel rides the keyboard's own animation; results cap lower while the keyboard is up.

## v1.33.0 — Personal Inbox, ticket titles in the Inbox, scroll-arrow root fix, Puzzle/Lore catalog (Nov 3, 2026)
- **Personal Inbox**: new `getInbox` action returns one mail-style list for the signed-in collector — every cash purchase order (Subject "Purchase Order", From the buyer), every trade offer (Subject "Trade Offer", From the proposer), and, for the owner only, every change-request ticket (Subject = its type title: Change Request / Bug / Idea, From the sender). Each item carries a preview (latest thread message, else order summary / ticket text), a status, and an unread count computed from the same `deal_thread_reads` markers as `getOrderMailStatus` (tickets: unread until `read_at` is stamped). The Home mail indicator now opens this Inbox window (its badge = total unread across orders + owner tickets); opening an order item lands on that order in Trades scrolled into view with its message thread auto-opened (which marks it read); opening a ticket item opens the tickets window focused on that ticket. The change-request form now offers only Bug and Idea (the "Change Request" type option was removed at Anthony's direction; the server still accepts the legacy kind for old tickets). Opened order cards (cash + trade) carry a mail-style header: Subject line + From line above the invoice. NOTE: the Inbox→ticket/order hand-off must NOT pop the Inbox's history entry first — the async popstate landed after the target opened and closed it (verified live in the probe; both hand-offs now close the Inbox silently and let the target push its own entry).
- **Scroll arrows — real root cause**: the v1.27 `ScrollArrows` measured the WINDOW (`window.scrollY`, `documentElement.scrollHeight`), but the app scrolls inside the shell's generated root div, so it concluded no page was ever long and never rendered — Anthony saw them nowhere. Rewritten to measure the real scroller (`findPageScroller`), listen in the capture phase, and observe `.app-shell`/`body` growth with a ResizeObserver. The down arrow also follows chunk-loaded pages (the binder grid loads pockets in batches): it re-aims at the bottom as the page grows until the height settles, so one tap reaches the true bottom. Verified visible and working in the probe on Home, Trades, the Rise of the Floodborn set page (full ride to the bottom), and Settings (both arrows, jump-row ride).
- **Puzzle / Lore catalog** (`server/src/data/puzzleCatalog.ts`, seeded through the normal catalog path — pieces are NOT in LorcanaJSON's allCards.json, checked Nov 3, 2026, which is why they were missing): per-set piece counts verified Nov 3, 2026 from marketplace listings, the Mushu Report wiki, and comicbook.com — S1 The First Chapter 4 (Mickey Mouse - Brave Little Tailor); S2 Rise of the Floodborn 4 (Minnie Mouse - Wide-Eyed Diver); S3 Into the Inklands NONE (rules cards instead — no Puzzle section); S4 Ursula's Return 9 (Ursula); S5 Shimmering Skies 4 (subject unverified); S6 Azurite Sea 6 (Stitch); S7 Archazia's Island 20 Lore cards (backs form two 9-piece pictures); S8 Reign of Jafar 19 Lore cards (sellers conflicted 19 vs 21; the "#1-19" listing + wiki excerpts to card 19 won — least certain count); S9 Fabled 9 (Powerline); S10 Whispers in the Well 18 Lore (Judy Hopps); S11 Winterspell 18 Lore; S12 Wilds Unknown 9 (modeled as Lore); S13 Attack of the Vine! SKIPPED — no verified count found anywhere; S14 Hyperia City 9 (Anthony's own figure). Each set also gets ONE "Full Puzzle Art" item (the assembled artwork as its own ownable). Only Floodborn's carries art: the WDWNT assembled photo, URL copied verbatim from Anthony's steer — https://r2-media.wdwnt.com/2023/11/lorcana-rise-of-the-floodborn-puzzle-3570.jpg (article: https://wdwnt.com/2023/11/first-look-disney-lorcana-rise-of-the-floodborns-12-enchanted-cards-minnie-mouse-puzzle/) — fetched into owned blob storage by the existing cardImage pipeline (blob `card-art/puzzle_2_full.jpg`). All other Full Puzzle Art items and every individual piece have no image (name-plate pockets) — no per-piece scan source was verified; nothing was hotlinked or guessed. Puzzle pockets are single (no Cold Foil stepper). Ownership note: Anthony's Shiny collection holds the complete Floodborn puzzle, ~6–7 copies of each piece (per his Nov 3 collection check) — the catalog rows start unowned because the Nov 1 export excluded puzzle pieces, and the exact per-piece count (6 or 7) was NOT guessed into his data; he can set the counts with the steppers.

## v1.34.0 — Standalone mail view, Inbox delete, deck builder, Welcome/registration, sideways dial, tile visibility (Nov 3, 2026)
- **Standalone mail view (supersedes v1.33's Inbox→Trades routing)**: Anthony's live report — tapping a message in the Inbox threw him into Social toward the bottom. Root cause: `openInboxOrder` closed the Inbox and navigated to Social/Trades with a focus hand-off. Now a message opens as its OWN page stacked above the still-open Inbox (z-95, own history entry): `CashPurchasesSection` gained an `onlyPurchaseId` single-item mode and `TradesPanel` an `onlyTradeId` early-return mode, so the mail view renders the real order card (Subject/From header, invoice, actions, thread + reply box) without any Social chrome. Its X (or phone Back) returns to the Inbox list. Ticket items stack the tickets window above the Inbox the same way. The Home mail badge and the Inbox read the same `getInbox` totalUnread (badge left of the envelope), fixing the missing Home count.
- **Inbox delete**: new `inbox_deletions` table (migration 0038) + `deleteInboxItem` (party-checked for orders, owner-only for tickets). Deleting is per-user like email: the order/trade/ticket is untouched, the other party keeps their copy, and because `getInbox` hides an item only while its latest-activity date is not newer than the deletion stamp, a new message in the thread makes it reappear. UI: trash + confirm on each Inbox row, and a trash next to the X in the standalone mail view with an explanatory confirm.
- **Deck builder**: new `decks` table (migration 0035: owner_code, name, cards_json, displayed, timestamps). Actions `getMyDecks`, `getDeckCandidates` (owned printings, name + trailing-number search, cap 80), `saveDeck` (server-validated: ≥1 card, ≤60 total, per-printing qty ≤ owned, ≤4 copies per card name across printings), `setDeckDisplayed`, `deleteDeck`. UI lives in the My Decks sheet (own profile): Create a deck → builder (name, search owned cards, steppers, running N/60), deck cards with an On display switch, Edit/Delete, and a read-only deck-list view. Availability hook: `deckCopiesByCard` now also sums saved-deck lists, so deck copies are held back wherever the existing "decks" trade-availability hold-back applies; deck building itself uses full owned quantities. The friend (Jarvis) sheet keeps its empty state — decks belong to the device collector and no friend-deck source exists.
- **Welcome page + registration + forced tour**: migration 0036 adds `collector_profile.welcome_seen` + `registered_at`. `getWelcomeState` reports needsWelcome and mode ("register" for a fresh profile — no registration, registry sync, or photo; "returning" for established). `checkUsername` validates format (3–20 letters/numbers/underscores, same rule as setCollectorUsername) and availability against the Supabase registry `profiles` (a name held by a different collector code is taken; own name is available; `reachable:false` when the registry can't be read). `registerCollector` re-checks, saves username + registered_at; the client follows with the existing `syncCollectorRegistry`. `completeWelcome` stamps welcome_seen once. Client `WelcomeFlow` (z-100): intro with the ring artwork and a blur-to-sharp CSS reveal (`.welcome-reveal` in theme.css, reduced-motion safe), the 4-step getting-started path; register mode = username form → Collector ID reveal → FORCED paged tour (7 pages, no skip/dismiss anywhere) → completeWelcome → Home; returning mode = dismissible, Get started completes it; Settings > About gains "See the welcome page again" (review mode, plain close).
- **Sideways count dial**: new shared `client/src/countDial.tsx` — long-hold on PLUS opens a horizontal 1–9 dial; tapping a number sets and saves the count immediately (no confirm). It replaces the old vertical 0–99 list in the card-detail finish picker and in `OwnedLocationBar` (which covers card, sealed, and comic details). The Sealed SHELF tiles previously had NO long-press wired on their steppers at all (the Deep Trove bug) — they now open the dial too, as does the Marketplace sell-form quantity stepper.
- **Search keyboard ghost pattern — diagnosed cause and fix**: the keyboard height lived in React state, so every visualViewport resize frame during the keyboard's rise re-rendered the whole app; the overlay also swapped its backdrop-blur off at focus (a full-screen repaint mid-animation) and the panel's translucent blurred surface smeared over the moving background. Now the overlay never uses backdrop-blur (solid dim), the keyboard height is a single boolean in state (two renders per open) with the pixel height written directly to the overlay's DOM node (rAF-throttled), and while the keyboard is up the panel is fully opaque with a plain shadow and no entrance animation. Panel position unchanged (pb-[26vh] ≈ 63% down). The harness has no software keyboard, so the animation itself can't be probe-verified — disclosed.
- **Your Collections tile visibility**: migration 0037 adds `collector_profile.hidden_collections` (JSON array of tile keys: lorcana, pokemon, magic, lego, comics, starwars). `getCollectionVisibility` / `setCollectionHidden` actions; Settings gains a "Your Collections" section (own jump icon, grid glyph) with one switch per tile, all shown by default; Home renders only unhidden tiles and the rest reflow centered three-across. Display-only — no data or totals touched.
- **Scroll arrows**: now translucent (surface at 35% + lighter border/shadow) and mirrored on BOTH the left and right edges (Anthony could see them after the v1.33 root fix and asked for the toning + both thumbs).
- **Great Hunny Rescue photo**: the item's photo was a bare 3D box render unlike the shelf standard. The current box art was placed (image edit of the real product photo) inside a clear acrylic display case on a black stand against black — the same presentation as the approved Hyperia City box photo — and installed as `client/src/assets/sealed/item-great-hunny-rescue.webp` (1600×1600, verified visually against the item).
- **Payment info jump icon — verified**: the Settings jump row is rendered unconditionally from the static `SETTINGS_JUMPS` array, which includes the "Payment info" banknote entry (`settings-payment`) — same always-rendered path as every other icon (the old shield flicker was a data-gated Admin shortcut, a different mechanism); re-verified in the probe with all jump icons present.

## v1.34.1 — Count dial opens above the thumb (Nov 3, 2026)
- Anthony live-tested the v1.34.0 sideways dial: it opened BELOW the + button, where the holding thumb covers it. Placement rule now: the dial renders directly ABOVE its stepper, in-flow, at all four call sites — card-detail finish rows (dial above the finishes list), the OwnedLocationBar Copies-owned bar on card/sealed/comic details (dial block moved to the top of the bar, border-b), the Sealed shelf tiles (dial + stepper row wrapped in one bottom-pinned block, dial first), and the Marketplace sell-form quantity (dial above the row). Behavior unchanged: tap a number, the count becomes that number and saves at once. The rule is recorded in countDial.tsx's header comment so future stepper sites place it above too.

## v1.35.0 — Swipe between bottom-bar tabs (Nov 3, 2026)
- **Requested by**: Anthony (~3:06 AM) — on Home, swipe left/right to move to the next tab (his example: slide a hand left to reach Search) instead of reaching down to tap the bottom menu.
- **Applied (client-only, App root)**: touch handlers on the app shell track a single-finger gesture; a clear horizontal swipe (≥64px sideways, ≥1.6× wider than tall — vertical scrolling and pull-to-refresh never qualify) moves one step in the bottom bar's own order: Home → Search → Sealed (Comics/Lego in those sections) → Social → Portfolio → Settings, and back. Search is a real stop: swipe right out of it closes the overlay (navigate's existing search hand-off), swipe left moves on to the next tab. The binder view counts as Home's slot (it has no bar tab of its own). The gesture never fires while a sheet/window/overlay is open (card, sealed, missing sheets, scanner, Inbox, mail view, tickets, update view, welcome), when the touch starts on a form field, or inside a horizontally scrollable strip (computed overflow-x auto/scroll ancestor), so chip rows and form controls keep their own gestures. Tab switches reuse `navigate`/`goSearch`, so history entries, haptics, and the search hand-off behave exactly like tapping the bar. Version 1.35.0 (minor: feature).
- **All-tabs clarification (Nov 3, ~3:14 AM steer)**: Anthony clarified the swipe is NOT a Home→Search shortcut — it tabs through ALL tabs in bottom-menu order, both directions, from wherever he is. That is how it was built (SWIPE_TAB_ORDER covers every stop; bounds stop at the first/last tab); no code change needed, confirmed against the clarification.
- **Animated swipe refinement (v1.35.1, Nov 3, ~3:13 AM steer)**: swipe-initiated tab switches now animate — `goSwipeTab` records the direction (next/prev by order index) and the App's `<main>` gets `.tab-slide-next` / `.tab-slide-prev` (theme.css: 240ms ease-out, 44px directional translate + crossfade from 35% opacity, reduced-motion safe), cleared on the main element's own animationend; the class is cleared and re-applied via requestAnimationFrame so rapid repeat swipes restart the slide. Tapping the bottom bar still cuts instantly — the animation belongs to the swipe, as directed. The Search overlay keeps its own entrance (search-pop) when a swipe opens it.
- **Count dial fixes folded into v1.35.0 (Anthony steers, Nov 3, ~3:08 AM — flagged as P0s by this build's audit)**: (1) Sealed shelf dial "only goes up to 4" ROOT-CAUSED — the shared dial renders 1–9, but the shelf tile's figure is `overflow-hidden` at ~156px of usable caption width against the dial's ~410px row, so 5–9 were clipped past the tile edge. `SidewaysCountDial` gained an `overlay` mode: the dial portals to the document body as a fixed full-width panel (≤350px, compact flex-1 number buttons) anchored just above the opening spot (measured from an in-flow anchor, re-measured on resize/scroll), so nothing can clip it. The width math showed the in-flow row (~410px) also exceeds the card sheet's phone width (~338px inside its padding) and the Copies-owned bar clips horizontally too (`overflow-hidden`), so overlay mode is used at ALL FOUR call sites — shelf tiles, card-detail finishes, the Copies-owned bar (card/sealed/comic details), and the Marketplace quantity — guaranteeing the full 1–9 in one row everywhere; the in-flow mode remains in the component but no call site uses it now. (2) Tap-outside-to-dismiss, per his dismiss rule: the dial component now installs capture-phase document listeners while mounted — a pointerdown outside the panel closes it without picking, and the follow-through click of that same tap is swallowed (stopPropagation + preventDefault within 700ms) so the tapped control does not also fire; Escape closes too. Applies at every call site. The long-press that opens the dial fires on a 450ms timer mid-press, so the opening press can never self-dismiss.

The builder carries the color swatches + featured-card picker; the shelf box embosses the deck name on its top band and shows the featured card in a bordered front window (art via the existing on-device art cache); tapping a box plays the lid-open animation (~1s, shorter under reduced motion) and lands on the deck page with per-card quantities. One detailed row per collector from the same live `getAdminStats` roster: username, Collector ID, Trust Score, items collected, confirmed connections, joined date. Server change: the roster gained a per-collector `connections` count — the admin connections fetch now selects `collector_a,collector_b,status` and counts CONFIRMED rows naming the collector on either side (same rule as the profile stats); null when the table can't be read, and the page then shows "Not available" rather than a made-up zero. Honest gaps labeled on the page itself: the registry stores a single username (no separate display name exists) and no last-active time is tracked, so neither is shown. Back is verified both ways: the page's Back button goes through history (closeSheet → history.back) and the phone back/gesture pops the same entry — both land on the Admin dashboard (the Home Admin window stays open beneath when entered from Home; the Settings Admin section when entered from Settings), never out of the app. Owner-only throughout: the page renders only inside `AdminDashboard`, which renders nothing for non-owners, and the server withholds the roster from every other profile. ### Sealed base-image acrylic audit (Nov 3, 2026)
**Requested by**: Anthony — in Sealed > All Available the not-owned ghost treatment looks perfect and must stay exactly as is, but he wants certainty that marking ANY catalog item owned shows the finished Illumineer's Quest: The Great Hunny Rescue standard: clean crystal acrylic presentation, no white/ghostly border baked into the photo. Audit every sealed catalog item's underlying base image (all boxes, packs, Quest boxes, all Illumineer's Troves, gift sets, starter decks, and the Gateway box, including unowned items) and amend any base image not already to that standard. No layout, sort, or toggle changes.
**Method**: all 63 catalog base images in `client/src/assets/sealed/` were inventoried against `SEALED_PHOTOS`. Each was measured programmatically (PIL/numpy) and visually on dark contact sheets composited exactly as the shelf renders them. The defect detector: neutral-white opaque pixels (min RGB channel > 230, channel range < 22, alpha > 200 — true studio-backdrop white, deliberately NOT matching cream/warm product packaging) reachable from the image border through transparent or same-white pixels, i.e. backdrop remnants outside the product silhouette. A looser near-white pass was also run to confirm that remaining light pixels on light-packaged products (Fabled and Archazia's Island decks/troves, Winterspell snow art, the Gateway box's own printed frame line) are the products' own packaging/art, enclosed by the product, not backdrop.
**Already to standard — not touched (49 items)**: all 16 full acrylic scenes whose case is baked into the photo (Hyperia City Booster Box, Hyperia City Prerelease Box, Illumineer's Quest: The Great Hunny Rescue itself, and all 13 booster packs: Hyperia City, Fabled, The First Chapter EN, The First Chapter JP, Winterspell, Wilds Unknown, Attack of the Vine!, Ursula's Return, Reign of Jafar, Shimmering Skies, Rise of the Floodborn, Into the Inklands, Azurite Sea) — detector found zero exterior backdrop white in every one; Illumineer's Quest: Palace Heist and Illumineer's Quest: Deep Trouble; all 7 gift sets (The First Chapter, Into the Inklands, Stitch Collector's, Archazia's Island Gift Box, Fabled, Winterspell Scrooge McDuck, Hyperia City Beast); all 23 starter decks / 2-player starter sets; and the Lorcana Gateway box (its thin inner frame is the box's own printed artwork, not a photo border). These render either as their baked acrylic scene or as a clean cut-out inside the CSS acrylic case, with no white/ghostly border in the base photo.
**Amended (14 items — every Illumineer's Trove)**: the Trove renders (CardsRealm catalog images, added Nov 3, 2026) still carried pieces of their white studio backdrop the original cut-out missed: full white strips along image edges (First Chapter Trove worst, 3.12% of all pixels), white bottom floor-strips under the box (Hyperia City, Reign of Jafar, Attack of the Vine!, Whispers in the Well, Wilds Unknown), white halo fringing (Archazia's Island, Fabled), and on the Winterspell Trove an opaque pure-grey shadow puddle baked under the box. Amendment was a deterministic pixel edit of the existing files only (no new external source, no regenerated art): border-connected neutral-white pixels within a shallow band of the silhouette were made transparent; the Winterspell puddle, being perfectly neutral grey (R=G=B) and exterior-connected, was removed the same way in a second pass. Removed per item: First Chapter 3.12%, Archazia's Island 2.03%, Fabled 2.01%, Winterspell 4.67% across both passes, Reign of Jafar 1.48%, Attack of the Vine! 1.39%, Whispers in the Well 1.34%, Wilds Unknown 1.12%, Hyperia City 1.04%, Rise of the Floodborn 0.13%, Shimmering Skies 0.11%, Azurite Sea 0.08%, Ursula's Return 0.06%, Into the Inklands 0.04%. Files re-encoded as WebP quality 93 at the same filenames and dimensions, so no code, mapping, layout, sort, or toggle changed.
**Verification**: detector re-run after amendment — exterior neutral-white is now ~0.00% on all 14 (residuals ≤0.13% are enclosed product highlights, unreachable from any border). Before/after dark contact sheets and native-size zooms (First Chapter, Archazia's Island, Reign of Jafar, Winterspell) confirm every strip, halo, and the puddle are gone and no product pixel was eaten: boxes, including cream-packaged ones, are intact. Owned-state consequence: a Trove marked owned now displays as a bare-box clean cut-out inside the CSS acrylic case (the same presentation as the two Quest cut-outs), and the ghost overlay for unowned items is untouched code (`.ghost-img` / `.acrylic-case img.ghost-img` in theme.css unchanged). Version bumped to 1.41.3 with a changelog entry.

## v1.42.0 — Hyperia City Ink Drop counter cards added (Nov 3, 2026)

- **Requested by**: Anthony — "new ink drops two new ink drops were introduced on this set I don't see those as well. Can you confirm that as well?" — confirmed missing (not in the LorcanaJSON-derived catalog, not in Lorcast, not in the binder), and the originating brief authorized adding them in advance if found missing.
- **What they are**: Hyperia City (Set 14) introduced the ink drop mechanic; its booster packs can put a printed Ink Drop counter card in the same last slot the puzzle pieces live in (Laughing Place prerelease-box review, https://www.laughingplace.com/disney-merchandise/disney-lorcana-hyperia-city-prerelease-box-review/ : Ink Drop cards "randomly appear in the last slot where different puzzle cards/advertisements/etc. have lived"). Exactly TWO designs exist, confirmed by TCGplayer's catalog through Anthony's own Shiny exports (all five exports, Oct 25 – Nov 1, 2026, list both): "Ink Drop" (TCGplayer 722932) and "Ink Drop (Blue)" (TCGplayer 722933).
- **Artwork (verified source)**: each design's real printed card scan from TCGplayer's own product CDN — https://tcgplayer-cdn.tcgplayer.com/product/722932_in_1000x1000.jpg and https://tcgplayer-cdn.tcgplayer.com/product/722933_in_1000x1000.jpg (548x763 JPEGs; fetched Nov 3, 2026 and visually verified: both are the genuine Counter cards, art by Matthew Eng, "EN - 14", rules text "(This counter doesn't count as a card.) You may remove this counter to pay 1 [ink]." — the gold design shows the bronze ink-drop device among crystals; the Blue design shows the silver device wreathed in blue energy at the M.A.G.I.C. academy). The URLs are stored as the entries' `image` in `server/src/data/puzzleCatalog.ts` and flow through the existing cardImage pipeline into owned blob storage on first view — no remote URL is rendered directly. (TCGplayer's product pages themselves would not load in the text browser; the CDN images were verified by direct fetch + visual inspection instead.)
- **Placement**: two hand-authored entries (`INK_DROP_ENTRIES` in puzzleCatalog.ts, uids `puzzle:14:inkdrop` / `puzzle:14:inkdrop-blue`, numbers 11/12) ride Hyperia City's existing puzzle group, so they render inside the Hyperia City set page's Puzzle section right after the Full Puzzle Art, as single pockets labeled "Counter" (pocketsOf now returns "Counter" for rarity "Counter"), ghosted until owned, with the set page's Puzzle caption noting them for set 14 only. seedBinder's catalog-additions path inserts them unowned on next load without touching any existing row or count — Anthony sets his own counts with the detail steppers, same as the puzzle pieces (his export token counts, 44 and 45, stay in the export's non-binder totals where they have always been counted; nothing was backfilled into ownership).

### Up-front artwork cache + background warm-up (Nov 3, 2026, v1.44.0)
**Requested by**: Anthony — set a rule for Vault X: download and cache ALL artwork images up front so users never wait for images to build/load as they browse. After first run (post-tour) and on app updates that add artwork, a background warm-up downloads the image catalog progressively — the user's own collection first, then the wider catalog set by set — storing to the existing on-device image cache; all views display cache-first. It must not block using the app. Show honest progress unobtrusively: a line in Settings > Your data & backups, "Artwork cache: X of Y images saved on this device." Refresh/replace cached images when artwork is updated (e.g. the sealed acrylic passes) so nobody gets stuck with stale art.
**Warm-up order implemented**: (1) the collector's own cards first (ownedQty > 0, in binder order — group order, then collector number), then (2) the rest of the catalog set by set in the same binder order (sets 1–14, then promo families, then quests/puzzle groups as their groupSort orders them). The order is computed server-side by the new `getArtworkWarmup` action, which returns the ordered card ids, the total count of printings that have artwork, the owned count, and the artwork generation string.
**Engine**: `startArtworkWarmup` in `client/src/artCache.ts` — starts from `App()` once the Welcome flow no longer shows (post-tour) and the plan has loaded; re-checked on every open, so an update that grows the catalog or bumps the artwork generation resumes the pass for exactly what's missing/fresh. Two downloads at a time through the existing shared `fetchSourceBlob` (the `cardImage` action path), a yield every few images, and an offline pause that gives up for the open and resumes next open — progress is the cache itself, so nothing is tracked twice. Triggering is idempotent and it never blocks rendering; all views were already cache-first via `useCachedArt`.
**What is cached**: the browsing (grid) artwork for every printing — the same 320px-wide JPEG the binder pockets and list rows display. Full-resolution detail art keeps the existing behavior (cached on first detail open, cap 120, backfills the grid copy): measured Nov 3, 2026, full-res Ravensburger scans are 1468x2048 at ~233–349 KB (six-sample average ~279 KB), so caching full-res for all 3,556 printings would cost roughly 1 GB per device, while the grid copies measured 33–41 KB each (three real downscales at quality 0.82: 33,109 / 40,281 / 41,398 bytes).
**Honest footprint**: a complete grid cache for the full catalog (3,556 printings with artwork, counted from the bundled catalog's image URLs) is about 3,556 x ~38 KB ≈ 135 MB on the device (say 120–150 MB allowing for art complexity). The Settings line also reports the real stored size from the cache index once images are saved. Sealed/comic/set-logo artwork is NOT in this cache and needs no warm-up: it ships inside the app bundle (~22 MB of assets) as content-hashed files.
**Stale-art refresh**: the card-art cache generation moved `lorcana-art-v1` → `lorcana-art-v2` (index key likewise), and the grid cap rose 1,600 → 4,200 so a full-catalog cache is never evicted. The server carries `ARTWORK_VERSION = "art-v2"`; the warm-up compares it to the generation last completed on the device and, on a mismatch, wipes the current cache and re-downloads — future artwork replacements (re-scans, restyle passes) bump `ARTWORK_VERSION` and the client's `CACHE_NAME` together. Bundled artwork (the sealed acrylic passes and friends) is replaced directly by the app update itself because its files are content-hashed in the build.
**Progress display**: Settings > Your data & backups, directly under the Cloud backup box — an "Artwork cache" block whose count line reads exactly "Artwork cache: X of Y images saved on this device." (X from the live on-device index, Y from `getArtworkWarmup`), plus the stored size in MB, a background-saving note while a pass runs, a "Save artwork now" resume button when a pass stopped short, and an all-saved note when complete.
**Version**: bumped to 1.44.0 with a changelog entry.

### Admin page load speed (Nov 3, 2026, v1.45.1)
**Requested by**: Anthony — tapping the Admin badge on Home sometimes took ~10 seconds before the Admin PAGE loaded (the badge itself was fine). Direction: the page should open immediately with its shell and headline numbers — serve the last-known stats snapshot instantly, labeled with its as-of time, refresh in the background, and/or load heavy aggregations progressively with honest loading states; never invented numbers while loading.
**Diagnosis (which queries made Admin slow)**: `getAdminStats` ran its phases strictly in series, so the page paid the SUM: (1) the Supabase `collection_items` read — 3,709 rows paged 1,000 at a time, one page after another (the single biggest slice); (2) bulk `connections` (limit 5,000) and `trust_events` (limit 5,000) reads plus the `profiles` read; (3) five local deal-flow queries; (4) the storage panel — four `supabaseTableEstimate` calls, each a count request followed by a separate 100-row sample request, plus the file-storage bucket listing; (5) ~35 `admin_metric_seen` writes awaited one by one. A dozen-plus sequential server round trips before the dashboard's single "Loading…" line could clear. (The badge-side prefetch into the shared ["adminStats"] cache sometimes masked this, which is why it was only SOMETIMES slow.)
**Applied — snapshot-first page**: new table `admin_stats_cache` (migration 0043, single row id=1). Every successful owner `getAdminStats` computation stores its full result there (best-effort upsert; the response also gained `computedAt`). New owner-gated action `getAdminStatsSnapshot` reads that one local row — no Supabase — and returns the stored payload plus its computation instant; a payload that no longer parses against the response schema reads as no snapshot. `AdminDashboard` now renders its shell at once: live `getAdminStats` data wins when present; until it lands, the snapshot stands in under a plainly worded banner ("Showing the last-known numbers from <time> — refreshing in the background…", or "Couldn't refresh just now — showing the last-known numbers from <time>" when the refresh fails); with live data, a small "Numbers as of <time>" line (plus "· Refreshing…" during a refetch) sits on top. With NO snapshot yet, the headline tiles and each section show honest "Loading… / Still computing" states — no placeholder numbers anywhere. Acking a changed metric now invalidates the snapshot query too.
**Applied — the computation itself is faster**: the local deal-flow queries and all five storage measurements now START alongside the registry reads and are only awaited where consumed (phases overlap instead of summing); collection_items pages fetch four at a time and stop at the first short page (a failed page still fails the whole items read — partial counts are never presented as real); each table estimate's count and sample requests run in parallel; the admin_metric_seen writes are applied as ONE drizzle batch.
**Verified in the sandbox**: `getAdminStatsSnapshot` on a fresh cache returned `{isOwner:true, computedAt:null, stats:null}` instantly (the honest no-snapshot state); a full `getAdminStats` returned the complete dashboard (2 collectors, 9,392 copies / 3,709 rows, storage 1.22 MB) with `computedAt` set; the snapshot read afterwards returned that exact payload with the matching computed-at instant. Owner gating unchanged on both actions (non-owner gets isOwner=false and no stats). Version bumped to 1.45.1 with a changelog entry.

### Admin page refresh-in-place (Nov 3, 2026, v1.45.2)
**Requested by**: Anthony — refreshing while on the ADMIN page threw him back to Home; refresh must reload the Admin page in place with its state intact. Sixth confirmed case of the refresh-loses-place family (Sealed main page, cash checkout, Illumineer's Quest 3 set view, View-my-profile, Library before it). Fix Admin specifically, verify on Admin specifically, and state whether the underlying mechanism is fixed globally or per-page.
**Answer on mechanism — PER-PAGE, and that is the app's actual architecture**: there is no single global refresh mechanism to fix. Each surface persists its own place: the main view restores from sessionStorage (`mycollectibles-view-v1`), the Social carts/checkout from sessionStorage (`vaultx-social-nav-v1`), and overlay sheets restore from the surviving history entry. The Admin window (Home shield badge) and its Registered Collectors sub-page were the surfaces still relying ONLY on the history-entry seed — and the shell's refresh reloads the page as a fresh navigation whose history state comes back empty, so both seeds read false on reload and the stack collapsed to Home.
**Applied**: new sessionStorage record `vaultx-admin-nav-v1` (same in-tab pattern as the checkout fix: survives an in-place reload, clears when the app fully closes, so a fresh open still starts clean). HomePanel's Admin window seeds from history OR the stored `homeOpen` flag and keeps the flag in step via an effect; `AdminDashboard` gained a `scope` prop ("home" for the Home window, "settings" for the Settings Admin section) and its Registered Collectors page likewise seeds from history OR its per-surface stored flag (`homeCollectorsOpen` / `settingsCollectorsOpen`) and keeps it in step — per-surface so restoring one never pops the page open on the other. In both, the history sheet counts as "seeded" only when the history entry itself carries the key, so a session-restored page closes by flipping state directly instead of stepping Back out of the app. The dashboard's numbers themselves were already refresh-safe (snapshot-first rendering, v1.45.1). No server, schema, or data changes.
**Version**: bumped to 1.45.2 with a changelog entry.

### Library Comics shelf sign — comic masthead treatment (Nov 3, 2026, v1.45.3)
**Requested by**: Anthony — fun styling: on the Library page, the "Comics" shelf label gets a fun treatment like the Promos shelf got its promo-sticker lettering, but in a COMICS idiom: classic comic-book masthead energy (bold punchy block lettering, comic-style offset shadow and/or halftone feel), unmistakably "comic books" the way the Promos sign is unmistakably "promos." Tasteful and readable at shelf-sign size, consistent with the Library's look; the Lorcana wordmark styling and the Promos sign stay as they are.
**Applied**: presentation only, in `LibraryPage` + `theme.css`. The Comics shelf sign is now its own branch beside the Promos branch: a `.comics-sign` plaque — deep comic blue (#1e40af family gradient) with a white halftone-dot layer (7px radial-gradient dots), a 1.5px near-black border, a hard 3px black offset shadow, and a slight -1.5deg tilt — carrying `.comics-masthead` lettering: "Comics" in 13px heavy block sans (Arial Black / Impact stack), uppercase, italic, skewX(-7deg), bright masthead yellow (#ffd81a) with a stacked black outline (text-shadow) and a red (#d92323) offset shadow over a black drop. Fixed comic colours in every app theme, like the Promos sticker. No other sign, shelf, spine, data, or action changed; the sign keeps an aria-label of "Comics" with the styled text aria-hidden. Version bumped to 1.45.3 with a changelog entry.

### Price alerts management page (Nov 3, 2026, v1.46.0)
**Requested by**: Anthony — in an item's detail view, the Price alerts box at the bottom: make the words "Price alerts" in that box TAPPABLE. Tapping opens a dedicated page listing ALL price alerts that user has set across every item — each row showing the item (art thumbnail + name), the alert they've set, and the item's current price. On that page the user can EDIT any alert (change its target) and DELETE any alert, in place. Back from that page returns to the item detail they came from (standing back rule). Alerts stay strictly per-user. If an alert's item is a sealed product or comic, it lists there too — one page for all of a user's alerts ("for a card or for an item").
**Applied — the box**: `PriceAlertBox` moved to the shared `client/src/priceAlerts.tsx`; its heading now reads "Price alerts" (plural) and the whole heading row is a button (with an "N set · All alerts ›" affordance) that opens the management page. The box itself is unchanged otherwise: it still sets alerts for its own item and lists/deletes that item's alerts inline.
**Applied — the page**: `PriceAlertsPage` in the same file — a full-screen layer over the item detail it was opened from. Rows come from the existing `getPriceAlerts` (now enriched server-side with `displayName` + `subtitle` per alert): card rows show the card's real art via the on-device art cache (`useCachedArt`, grid copy), sealed rows the product's own photo (`SEALED_PHOTOS` resolver passed from App), comic rows the slab's real cover (COMICS resolver). Each row shows the alert ("at or below / at or above $X", flagged "triggered now" with the red treatment when live) and the item's current price ("Now" column; comic values carry the "~" estimate prefix like everywhere else). Triggered alerts sort first, then alphabetical. Edit opens an in-place direction + threshold form on the row (new `updatePriceAlert` action; the item itself never changes, only the watched target); Delete removes the alert in place (existing `deletePriceAlert`). Honest empty state when the user has no alerts; loading and error-with-retry states included. No alerts are seeded or invented.
**Applied — back behavior**: the page is its own history layer using the merged-state pattern (like `useHistorySheet`): opening pushes one entry merging the underlying detail's entry plus `lorcanaPriceAlerts: true`; the detail sheets it can sit over (card in App, sealed in App, comic in comics.tsx) now also merge their entries on open and only close on popstate when their own key is gone from the landed entry — so Back (page button, Close, or device gesture) pops ONLY the alerts layer and lands on the exact item detail it came from. A refresh on the open page reopens it in place: the box seeds open from the surviving entry, and the card/comic sheets seed their Performance tab (where the box lives) from the same entry.
**Applied — comics join the alerts system**: graded comics previously had no price-alert box at all. `price_alerts` gained a nullable `comic_id` column (migration 0044); `setPriceAlert` accepts targetKind "comic" with a comicId validated against a new server-side `COMIC_ALERT_CATALOG` in actions.ts mirroring the client COMICS catalog (same 8 slabs, same Oct 29, 2026 checked values — estimates, not live prices); `getPriceAlerts` resolves comic current values and triggered state from it. ComicSheet's Performance tab now carries the same Price alerts box (watching the slab's checked value), and a triggered comic alert's Home banner now taps through to the Comics view.
**Per-user**: alerts were and stay rows in the app's own per-user database behind these actions; the page only ever reads/writes the signed-in user's own alerts — no other collector's alerts are reachable or shown.
**Cleanup in passing**: the orphaned helpers of the removed standalone Sealed shelf (`SealedTypeFilter`, `SealedSortKey`, `sealedTypeOf`, `ThemedSelect`) and the intentionally-null `focusDeal` setter were keeping the client typecheck red; the dead declarations were removed (no behavior change — nothing referenced them).
**Verified in the sandbox**: setPriceAlert created card (Elsa - Spirit of Winter, below $5 → triggered at $4.76), sealed (Hyperia City Booster Box, above $150 → not triggered at $138.13), and comic (Batman #136, below $350 → not triggered at $400) alerts; getPriceAlerts returned all three with correct displayName/subtitle/currentValue/triggered; updatePriceAlert flipped the comic alert to "above $350" and it re-read as triggered; the test alerts were then deleted. Version bumped to 1.46.0 with a changelog entry.

### Copy-a-deck + collection check (Nov 3, 2026)
**Requested by**: Anthony (Nov 3, 2026): when viewing ANOTHER user's deck (a deck box on display on that user's shelf/profile), tap it and COPY it, checked against your OWN collection. (1) The copy lands in the copier's My Decks, attributed ("Copied from [owner]"). (2) An analysis runs the deck's list against the copier's collection using full owned quantities (the deck-building rule, not trade availability): headline "You have X of 60 — you need Y more" (rendered against the deck's actual total), then a per-card breakdown (deck needs N copies, you own M, shortfall), and a buy list of just the missing copies. (3) Consistent with the set tiles' "Cost to complete," the estimated cost of the missing copies at current prices where priced, honestly unpriced where not. Only decks the owner has on display can be copied. Back navigation returns to the deck they were viewing. Extension (same day): the missing cards are actionable — a "Send buy list to my Muse" action (whole list or per-card) hands the user's OWN Muse a ready-to-execute instruction (exact missing cards: name, set, collector number, quantity needed, printing/TCGplayer id) directing it to build a TCGplayer cart on that user's account.
**Schema**: migration 0045 adds `copied_from_code`, `copied_from_username`, `source_deck_name` to `decks` (null on self-built decks). `deckView` carries the three fields; the My Decks list and deck page render "Copied from [username]".
**Actions**: `getDisplayedDecks` (only `displayed = 1` decks for a collector code — the display gate lives server-side), `copyDeck` (refuses own decks and non-displayed decks; inserts the copy with attribution, NOT validated against the copier's owned quantities — copying a deck you don't fully own is the point; starts off display; returns the collection check), `checkDeck` (re-runs the check for any deck list, incl. your own decks from their deck page), `requestDeckBuyList` (the Muse hand-off, below). Check math: `checkDeckLines` uses `cards.ownedQty` in full; unit price per missing printing via `printingUnitPrice` (Regular tracked price → any tracked finish price → Lorcast snapshot by TCGplayer id → stored weighted market value → null, disclosed as unpriced). Jarvis's displayed deck: the built-in test friend is the only other collector in the app, and his side of every social feature runs on his known stock — his one displayed deck ("Jarvis Test Deck") is built server-side from exactly his three known printings at his known quantities (Mickey Mouse - Brave Little Tailor ×1, Elsa - Snow Queen ×1, Stitch - Rock Star ×2, mirroring JARVIS_TRADE_STOCK / JARVIS_FRIEND_CARDS), resolved to catalog ids by exact name; its view id is the sentinel -1 so `copyDeck` can tell it from a stored row. No cards beyond his known stock were invented for it.
**Muse hand-off — what was actually built (mechanism honesty)**: Vault X cannot reach into a user's Muse or TCGplayer account by itself. `requestDeckBuyList` uses the app's one real hand-off channel — the same `ctx.agent.spawnTask` the set completion "Buy missing cards" button already uses — to hand the signed-in collector's own Muse (Jarvis, in this app) a ready-to-execute instruction naming the exact missing cards (name, set, collector number, quantity, TCGplayer id, market price) with the direction to build the cart on TCGplayer under the collector's own sign-in, under Anthony's standing TCGplayer rules (Near Mint only, no single-card package over $3 shipping, optimize sellers) and never to check out — final payment approval stays with the collector. The in-app copy on the check view labels it exactly that way ("Vault X can't open TCGplayer or buy anything itself…"). This matches the brief's "prepared instruction the user's Muse picks up from the app context" in effect — the receiving Muse does the TCGplayer work — with the delivery being the app's agent-task channel rather than a clipboard/share hand-off.
**Client**: `DecksSheet` takes `ownerCode`; a friend's page loads `getDisplayedDecks` (loading state, honest empty state unchanged); the deck view on someone else's deck carries the "Want this deck?" copy panel; success lands on `DeckCheckView` (headline, breakdown, buy list, per-card + whole-list Send buttons with honest explainer text), whose Back returns to that deck's view (standing back rule); your own deck view gains "Check against my collection" into the same view. Copied decks show their attribution in Your decks and on their deck page, and can be re-checked any time. Version 1.47.0.

### Catalog audit-and-fill + daily catalog audit (Nov 3, 2026)
**Requested by**: `All of them. I want every card that Lorcana has come out with in our catalog obtained to scan. And to cross-reference with and to add.` + the catalog completeness rule and daily-audit directive recorded the same day (audit at least once a day, ongoing; existing cards, ownership, and prices untouched).
**Audit result**: the bundled catalog (LorcanaJSON snapshot, 3,556 cards) is complete for main sets 1–14, Illumineer's Quests Q1–Q3, and the P1/P3/PD1/D23/CC1/RPH promo families, but the SOURCE itself truncates Challenge C2 at #10 and lacks later promo printings. The live LorcanaJSON file (https://lorcanajson.org/files/current/en/allCards.json, re-fetched Nov 3, 2026) still holds exactly 3,556 cards, so the gaps were filled from two further tested sources:
- **DL Promos** (https://dlpromos.ink/sets; per-set pages https://dlpromos.ink/set/c1, https://dlpromos.ink/set/c2, https://dlpromos.ink/set/c3, https://dlpromos.ink/set/cc2, https://dlpromos.ink/set/dis, https://dlpromos.ink/set/p2, https://dlpromos.ink/set/p4) — the complete promo reference, organized by the suffix printed on the card, with per-language variants, so English-only printings could be told apart from Japanese/Chinese-only ones (P1 #25/#41, P2 #38/#42/#43/#44, P3 #59/#61–63, P4 #7/#8 are NOT English printings and were not added; neither was the 1-of-1 serialized 2025 World Championship Mickey Mouse - Brave Little Tailor that carries no C1 number).
- **Lorcast API** (https://api.lorcast.com/v0/sets; https://api.lorcast.com/v0/cards/search?q=set:C2 and set:cp / set:DIS / set:P4) — cross-check of the Challenge families and the stats source for Down In New Orleans, which has no main-set printing in LorcanaJSON. A third cross-check: the Card Rodeo checklist https://cardrodeo.com/api/checklist?game=lorcana&set=lorcana-challenge-year-3&format=pdf-text (C2 = 14 distinct cards; Tempest-foil and plain printings are separately numbered, the same model LorcanaJSON uses for C2 #1–10).
**Added (40 printings, `server/src/data/catalogExtras.ts`, uids `x:<FAMILY>:<number>`)**: C1 +1 (5/C1 Mickey Mouse - Brave Little Tailor, the serialized Challenge Season 1 prize); C2 +10 (11–14 Tempest-foil and 15–18 plain Stand Out / Down In New Orleans / The Headless Horseman - Cursed Rider / Tinker Bell - Insistent Fairy, 19/C2 Tempest-foil and 20/C2 plain Minnie Mouse - Amethyst Champion); C3 +17 (the whole released family: 1–5, 11, 12, 13–17, 23, 24, 28A, 28B, 29 — numbers 6–10, 18–22, 25–27 are not released yet); CC2 +6 (the whole Beauty and the Beast Curator's Collection); DIS +2 (5/DIS Baymax - Lab Assistant, 19/DIS Creative Inspiration); P2 +3 (15/P2 Mickey Mouse - True Friend, 16/P2 Elsa - Queen Regent, 17/P2 Anna - Braving the Storm); P4 +1 (1/P4 Donald Duck - Taxi Driver). Stats were copied from each card's main-set printing in LorcanaJSON (promo printings are reprints with identical game text); Hades - Dark Usurper (C3-exclusive, home set 15 unreleased) had its stats read off its DL Promos card scans (cost 8, Sapphire, 10/10, 2 lore) and its ink confirmed on its DL Promos page. Artwork is the real English card scans hosted by DL Promos (https://lorcana.ngtj.com/storage/card-variant-images/en/5-c1-en.jpg is the 5/C1 example; every one of the 40 URLs was fetched and returned HTTP 200, and samples were viewed to confirm they depict the named printing — e.g. the 11/C2 Stand Out scan shows "11/C2 • EN • 9" and the 20/C2 Minnie scan matches Anthony's own photographed copy); the existing cardImage action fetches each scan into owned blob storage on first view. The three P2 additions carry TCGplayer ids taken from their mirrored TCGplayer product-image filenames at the same source (616373 / 647091 / 636354); the rest price as unpriced catalog cards until an id is backfilled.
**Delivery into existing binders**: new actions `syncCatalogAdditions` (inserts only bundled catalog entries whose uid has no row yet — no ownership or price field of any existing row is touched; declared as this build's post-submit action so the production binder gains the 40 printings plus any other bundled entries it was missing, including the Hyperia City Ink Drop counters in the puzzle data), `getCatalogIndex`, and `saveCatalogAdditions` (the audit task's save path — inserts new uids only, duplicates skipped). seedBinder's additions path now covers the extras too via `fullCatalog`. FAMILY_ORDER gained C3 and CC2 appended at the end so stored group sorts never shift.
**Daily audit**: managed cron `lorcana-binder-daily-catalog-audit` (daily 07:15 America/Chicago) calls `refreshCatalogAudit`, which spawns an agent task (dedupeKey per day, expectsAction saveCatalogAdditions) instructed to diff getCatalogIndex against LorcanaJSON + DL Promos + Lorcast and save verified missing English printings — the same one-retrieval-path pattern as the daily re-pricing. Version 1.49.0.

## Follow-up pass (Nov 3, 2026, v1.50.0)

**Promo prices** (`server/src/data/promoPrices.ts`): Lorcast API per-family pulls (https://api.lorcast.com/v0/cards/search?q=set:P1 / P2 / P3 / P4 / PD1 / D23 / DIS / C2 / CC1 / cp, pulled Nov 3, 2026), matched to binder uids by family + collector number + card name (Challenge Tempest/plain pairs share Lorcast's single record). 131 of 255 promo printings priced; Lorcast tracks many event/store promos with no price (not sold on TCGplayer) and does not carry C3/CC2/RPH at all — those stay honestly unpriced and are disclosed as "no tracked price" in the completion views. Wired as a uid-keyed fallback in pocketPrice / cardFinishMarketValue / printingUnitPrice, which is what computes the tiles' Cost to complete. Promo tiles now also render the progress bar with owned/total counts, matching chapter tiles; when no missing pocket in a family carries any tracked price, the tile line reads "Cost to complete · no tracked prices yet" instead of $0.

**Catalog audit log** (`catalog_audit_log`, migration 0046): saveCatalogAdditions writes one row per completed audit save (added + skipped counts); the daily task is instructed to call it once per run, with an empty list when nothing is new, so every run is recorded. `getCatalogAuditStatus` serves the newest row to the "Catalog audit" block in Settings > Your data & backups, which also states the audit's scope honestly (published English printings from LorcanaJSON/Lorcast/DL Promos; unannounced/unreleased and non-English-only printings are out of scope).

**Own-profile refresh-in-place**: the Collector ID / own profile / Library stack now also persists in sessionStorage (`vaultx-profile-nav-v1`), the same pattern as the Admin fix — the shell's pull-to-refresh reload returns an empty history state, which was the whole cause of the profile disappearing to Home and coming back.

**Dropdowns**: all ten native `<select>` elements (profile Viewing/sort/filter, friend-profile set filter, marketplace set/sort, deck featured card, listing condition, price-alert direction ×2) replaced by one shared in-app component (`client/src/vxSelect.tsx`) — dark rounded panel, accent check on the selected row; options and handlers unchanged.

**Slab labels** (comics.tsx): the grade box is now an equal square holding only "9.8" on every slab; "WHITE Pages" was removed from under the grade on Universal (non-signature) labels, and the Signature Series books print it on the label's centered line instead, like Aliens #1's older label. The books' page-quality data in the detail text below the slab is unchanged.

**Library shelf placement**: the profile shelf's horizontal center is now the midpoint between the profile photo's right edge and the deck-box icon's far corner (column 50% − 6px, was 50% − 42px center-to-center); vertical placement untouched.

**Village Aggro deck creation** (Anthony, Nov 3, 2026): the locked 60-card list lives at `~/workspace/lorcana/deck_village_aggro.json` (user-supplied name -> copies) and is bundled verbatim in `server/src/data/villageAggroDeck.ts`. `createVillageAggroDeck` (declared as this build's post-submit action) resolves each name at runtime against the live binder to a printing he owns enough copies of (Amber/Emerald printing preferred — the deck is Amber/Emerald — then most-owned, then lowest id), creates the deck on display, and assigns each used quantity a "Where it lives" row (deck_box, place "Village Aggro"); it is idempotent (existing deck of that name = report, change nothing) and fails whole, naming the card, when a printing cannot be resolved. The deck page shows its value from his own inventory (`deckView.inventoryValue`: each line's copies at the value his collection carries for that printing, falling back to the tracked Regular finish price; unpriced copies counted in `unpricedCards`, never priced at buy-it-new cost). Sandbox-verified before shipping: 60 cards, inks Amber/Emerald only, inventory value $91.46, all 20 lines resolved, location rows created, second call a no-op. The same stored-profile-nav fix that keeps the profile and Library in place on refresh now also covers My Decks (decksOpen), the eighth case of the refresh-loses-your-place family.

## v1.51.0 — steer batch (Nov 3, 2026, ~4:44–4:58 PM)

**Scanner timeout + diagnosis** (Anthony's two failed live scans of Minnie 20/C2, 38s and 30s+, both no-result): diagnosis by code inspection — the 20/C2 catalog row is complete and correct, and the identifyCard matcher yields a definite match or pickable candidates for any plausible vision read of that card (exact-name score 100; collector number is a hard filter; same-name ties go to candidates). Search reads the same rows and finds her, so catalog reachability is proven. The only path to a completely EMPTY result is the single server vision call (ctx.inference.complete) returning zero/unrelated cards — that call was the slow, fragile step (ceilings were 75s server / 45s client). Fix shipped: BOTH ceilings are now a hard 10 seconds (Anthony's directed 5–10s band, replacing the 45s ceiling); on timeout or a failed read the scanner lands on the no-match screen WITH its catalog search (new EMPTY_SCAN_RESULT + identifyFailed state) instead of an error line or an endless spinner; the vision prompt now explicitly reads promo collector lines (20/C2, 34/P2). The full on-device rebuild (precomputed index over the catalog artwork + collector-line OCR, ~1s target, per the approved research direction — Ximilar rejected by Anthony) is a dedicated follow-on build, NOT part of this pass.

**Swipe-to-tab removed** (Anthony: works, doesn't like it): the app shell's touch handlers, the swipe state machine, the pageAnim slide/crossfade state and its CSS (.tab-slide-next/.tab-slide-prev/.tab-fade) are all deleted; tab taps are instant cuts. The Library flip-binder's finger-swipe page flipping is a separate feature and was NOT touched (its data-tab-swipe-lock markers remain, now inert).

**Active-tab bubble**: the bottom bar's active highlight went from rounded-[20px] (the rejected in-between oval) to rounded-[10px] — a boxier rounded-corner rectangle hugging icon + label; the blue-purple accent is unchanged.

## v1.52.0 (Nov 3, 2026): trade/cash auto-settlement, deck back, refresh cover, Admin readable changes

- **Auto-settlement** (Anthony, Nov 3, 2026, ~5:17 PM — his directed
  behavior): when a trade is FULLY complete (accepted + both sides
  confirmed + both feedbacks in, or the 7-day neutral close), the agreed
  give/get lists settle both collections automatically; same for a
  completed cash purchase (seller → buyer). Migration 0047 adds
  `settled_at` + `settlement_json` to trade_offers and cash_purchases.
  Engine (actions.ts): the first attempt computes a plan of ABSOLUTE
  targets — per-card finish maps for the local binder; absolute
  collection_items rows for the counterparty (their collection of
  record is Supabase) — and stores it in settlement_json; application
  writes cloud targets first (absolute upserts/deletes, replay-safe),
  then local card updates + the settled_at stamp in ONE db.batch, then
  syncCardToCloud mirrors. A failed cloud write leaves settled_at null
  and the stored plan replays verbatim — never a double-apply, never a
  partial local move. Triggers: the second side's feedback submission
  (submitTradeFeedback / submitCashFeedback) plus a settle sweep on
  getTrades / getCashPurchases reads (covers the neutral close).
  Local deductions clamp to available copies of the exact
  printing+finish; paid/value figures untouched; received copies get
  NO item_locations row (unassigned) — the deal row + stored plan
  (with its applied record and "Trade #N"/"Cash purchase #N" source
  note) is the source record. Sandbox proof (Nov 3): trade #1 moved
  Abuelita Regular 12→11 / ownedQty 14→13 for Anthony and Stitch -
  Rock Star Regular 1→2 after BOTH feedbacks (quantities provably
  unchanged after confirms alone and after one feedback); cash
  purchase #5 then moved Stitch 2→3; re-reads did not double-apply.
  UI: a settled line on the trade/cash cards and Anthony's disclaimer
  as small print at the very bottom of the trade card: "Once this
  trade is complete, the new quantities will be reflected in your
  sets — there's nothing further for you to do."
- **Deck-detail back** (Anthony, ~5:24 PM): opening a deck pushed no

  detail/builder/check opens; Back (header arrow, phone back, the

  OwnProfilePage renders only once getCollectorId data lands, so Home

  from the first frame whenever a restored profile stack is open and
  the profile query is still pending; it lifts the moment data (or an

- **Admin changes, readable + instant-clear** (Anthony, ~5:26–5:30
  PM): two fixes. (1) Tapping a highlighted metric now clears it in

  highlight computation reads at once (the server ack still fires);
  previously the highlight only cleared when the slow getAdminStats
