HOME FIRST PAINT (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): HomePanel holds a themed cover (ring artwork + skeleton) once per session until collectionVisibility + finance + priceAlerts settle (errors settle too), then reveals the complete screen in one paint; later Home returns render from cache immediately.

MARKETPLACE BUILD-OUT (Anthony approved the mockup design Nov 4, 2026; built behind the EXISTING release gate — MARKETPLACE_AVAILABLE stays false and MarketplaceHeldPanel remains the user-facing surface in Social; nothing about payments, fees, who-can-sell, or shipping was decided, so none of it is implemented): design source is the approved static artifact marketplace-ideas-mockups (browse feed with filter chips, listing detail with seller card beside Buy, per-seller shop page, sell screen with live vs-market line and Allow offers switch default off, checkout = the existing purchase-order flow). Changes: marketplace_listings.allow_offers (migration 0054, default 0 = fixed price only); placeOffer rejects listings with offers off; createListing takes allowOffers; updateListing can flip it while available; getSellerShop action returns a seller's live listings + confirmed-sales count (Trust Score still read client-side from the registry, same as the rest of the panel); completed card deals now auto-settle through the trades/cash settlement machinery (marketplace_offers.settled_at + settlement_json, settleMarketplaceIfReady, triggered on the second feedback and swept on Inbox reads; sealed/comic listings carry no binder card row, so only card deals move copies). Client: ShopView ("<name>'s Shop", avatar, Trust/confirmed sales/listings live, kind chips + Under $50), ListingTile gained one-tap Buy and a tappable seller line that opens the shop, ListingSheet gates the offer UI on allowOffers and shows the mockup's fixed-price line otherwise plus a Visit shop button, SellSheet gained the Allow offers switch (default off) and a live asking-vs-market line while pricing, Your Listings gained a per-listing Allow offers switch, browse gained the Under $50 chip and the mockup's closing line. The near-you chip from the mockup was NOT built: listings carry no seller location data to ground it.

HOME LAUNCH ROOT CAUSE + SNAPSHOT (Anthony re-report Nov 4, 2026): the v1.74 cover gated on the in-memory react-query cache, which is empty on every cold launch, so cache-first almost never hit and the cover waited on the network each launch; additionally the Collector ID pill at the top of Home ran two more unseeded queries (collectorId, registryMembers) that popped in after the reveal. Fix: an on-device Home snapshot in localStorage (vaultx-home-snapshot-v1) persisting the last-seen payloads of all six Home-surface queries (collectionVisibility, finance ~15KB, priceAlerts, gameCatalog funko, collectorId, registryMembers), seeded via initialData + initialDataUpdatedAt (the binder cache's proven pattern) with write-back effects whenever fresh data lands — first frame is the complete last-seen Home, revalidation swaps quietly behind the identical layout, and the themed cover remains only for a true first-ever run.

## v1.76.0 — Portfolio Track & Research, theme-top fix, puzzle art, stepper shrink, Settings cleanup (Nov 4, 2026)

PORTFOLIO TRACK & RESEARCH (Anthony approved the finished mockup "portfolio-watchlist-alerts-research-mockup" — "Go ahead and put this through"): a new client module (client/src/portfolioTools.tsx) rendered inside FinancePanel directly below the hero block that carries the Re-price / Re-price Force pills (the pills and the hero stay completely icon-free, per spec). ONE pill-format dropdown on the shared themed recipe (dark surface, rounded, accent ring when open — never native): rows Watchlist (eye), Alerts (bell + red "N triggered" badge when any alert has triggered), Research (chart), each with a short description; selecting swaps the area below, all inside Portfolio. WATCHLIST: new watchlist_items table (migration 0055) + getWatchlist / addToWatchlist / removeFromWatchlist actions; rows are quote-style (art thumb, name, set line, owned state, canonical current price, day change from the tracked history, SVG sparkline of the last 14 history points); items are added via the new WatchToggleButton on card and sealed detail views (beside the Price alerts box); a bell on each row opens an inline alert setter that writes through the same setPriceAlert action as the detail pages; the × stops watching. ALERTS: the unified management home for the existing price alerts — the list was extracted from the management page into an exported PriceAlertsList in priceAlerts.tsx (same AlertRow, same getPriceAlerts data, triggered first, Edit/Delete in place) and renders here; creation still happens on item detail. RESEARCH: getResearchCard action returns one card's canonical price + TCGplayer id, full price history (table with the bundled-export fallback, same as getCard), the eBay sold snapshot figure with window + last-sold month + staleness when one exists (pricing order TCGplayer #1, eBay sold #2; the market-compare card shows "last sold at $X on eBay" with the recorded window and an over-90-days last-sold warning; cards with no recorded eBay sales say so honestly), the position (owned qty, average paid from real finance, totals, net), every other printing of the same card name with price/owned (tap to research it), and set-completion context (pockets owned/total, cost to complete from missingPocketsOf, unpriced-missing count). Picker: VxSelect over watchlist + alert cards, plus a search box wired to searchCards. No sample data anywhere; every area has honest empty/error states.

THEME COLOR AT THE TOP + SEARCH TRANSITION (Anthony's own diagnosis, Nov 4, 2026): the document now carries a theme-color meta (client/index.html, default Vault Dark bg) that main.tsx sets from the stored theme before first paint and themes.ts applyTheme updates on every theme change to the ACTIVE theme's exact --bg as painted in theme.css (new THEME_BG table — Rainbow's painted bg is #0b0f1e, a touch different from its picker swatch). The frame color is a document-level value, identical on every page/tab, so the band around the header can no longer flip between Home (light) and Search (themed). Search transition: the overlay root now fades in as one piece (new .search-fade, 180ms ease-out) and the panel settle shrank from the old 0.62s rise+scale to a 260ms small rise with no scale — the settled Search state is unchanged (heading + box at top, keyboard only on tap); no swipe navigation reintroduced.

RAINBOW THEME SWEEP (Anthony re-report, Nov 4, 2026): under Rainbow, --gold is the accent gradient's blue end (#4f8cff), so every gold-token FILLED control rendered flat blue and read as the old Vault theme — his named cases: the change-request Bug/Idea toggle + Send button, the Check for update button, the About box. Fix at the token/rule level in theme.css: the existing Rainbow gradient rule (accent-filled controls take the blue-to-violet Trades gradient + glow) now also covers bg-[var(--gold)] controls, with white text like the Trades gradient buttons; and --gold-soft under Rainbow moved from light blue #bcd4ff to violet-leaning #c9b8ff so soft-accent text (the About box buttons) sits in the Rainbow world. Remaining literal blues in the client are intentional semantic highlights (unread-count badges, "new" markers in admin/inbox lists, chart stops), not theme chrome.

HYPERIA CITY FULL PUZZLE ART (Anthony live report: pieces fine, full art completely wrong): investigation — the shipped catalog data for Hyperia's Full entry (puzzle:14:full) carries NO photo at all; the entry renders an assembled composite of its own nine piece scans, and the piece order was verified correct against Hoard's TCGplayer-fed per-piece pages (724311 Top Left, 724312 Top Middle, 724313 Top Right, 724314 Middle Left, 724315 Middle Center, ascending ids = reading order; https://www.tryhoard.com/market/lorcana/14). The wrong photo on Anthony's device was therefore a stale on-device cached image from an earlier build's catalog state (the art cache serves the device copy first and the server row now carries no image to replace it). Two fixes: (1) the Full entry now carries the GENUINE assembled photo — the "Set of 9" product 723665 DOES have its assembled-artwork photo on TCGplayer's CDN (Hoard's listing shows product 723665's photo for "Hyperia City Puzzle Insert (Set of 9)"; the catalog's earlier no-image note was wrong) — puzzleCatalog set 14 gains fullArtTcg: 723665, and the seedBinder puzzle sync propagates the new imageUrl and drops the stale blob; (2) the artwork cache generation bumped art-v2 -> art-v3 (server ARTWORK_VERSION + client CACHE_NAME lorcana-art-v3), so every device wipes the old art cache on the next warm-up and re-downloads fresh — no stale wrong image can linger for ANY entry. The composite remains the render fallback if a photo fetch fails. Other sets audited: sets 1, 2, 4, 6, 9, 11, 12, 13 carry real assembled photos from their own Set-of-N products (or the verified WDWNT photo for set 2), sets 5/7/8/10 remain honestly imageless name plates (no assembled image exists in any source; lore sets' pictures live on card backs) — no other entry was wrong or stuck.

STEPPER RING SHRINK (Anthony: the hollow glow is perfect, the circle is too big and spills past pocket borders): only the VISIBLE ring shrank; the glow recipe, the ~56px invisible touch area, and the long-press 1-9 dial are untouched. Pocket stepper row (two-across grids + sealed tiles): ring 40px -> 32px. Card detail finish rows and the marketplace Sell sheet: 32px -> 28px. Game sections (Pokemon/Magic/Star Wars/Funko): grid steppers 44px -> 32px, detail stepper 40px -> 32px.

SETTINGS — YOUR COLLECTIONS SECTION DELETED (Anthony: the control already exists on Home with the eye): the whole Settings section (heading, icon, dropdown box) is removed, along with its entry in the Settings section list and the now-dead CollectionsDropdownBox component. The Home eye button beside "Your Collections" is the single control; its window renders CollectionsVisibilitySwitches over COLLECTION_TILE_OPTIONS — all seven collections (Lorcana, Pokemon, Magic: The Gathering, Legos, Comics, Star Wars Unlimited, Funko). Behavior unchanged: hidden tiles vanish, the rest reflow centered three-across, preference persists on the profile.

FUNKO TILE ARTWORK — ANTHONY'S PICK (Nov 4, 2026): he reviewed three options in chat and chose Option 1, "The Shelf" (three cased vinyl figures — lavender cat-hood with star, blue astronaut with moon, purple horned with skull — on a shelf, blue-purple glow). The picked file (workspace/imagine_media/media-generation-funko-option-1-shelf-0-77818644-5db1-47e2-83d9-f02729f9986f.webp) could not be byte-copied into the artifact (the sandbox zeroes binary reads of it), so it was reproduced through the media pipeline with the pick as the reference image and verified visually to match (same figures, cases, shelf, glow, framing): client/src/assets/media-generation-collection-funko-shelf-0-5a72fde2-204f-4a5e-bcb0-1b87f3f6f1d2.webp, now the collectionFunko import (Home tile + Funko section header, which reuses the tile art). COMING SOON LABEL — HONEST DISCREPANCY, reported rather than papered over: Anthony reported the tile's yellow label missing and directed it stay until the catalog lands. Verified against the shipped data this build: getGameCatalog for funko returns complete = true with 23,939 figures across 90+ sections and the Funko section is browsable — the catalog HAS landed (it completed with the v1.74 post-submit import), so under Anthony's own graduation rule (label on until the catalog is live and browsable, off once the tile opens the real section — the same rule Pokemon/Magic/Star Wars followed) the label is correctly OFF and was not forced back on; forcing it would have mislabeled a live section as coming soon. The changelog tells him plainly, with the count.

MARKETPLACE GATE: untouched — MARKETPLACE_AVAILABLE remains false; nothing about payments, fees, seller eligibility, or shipping was built.

## v1.77.0 — Verified badge + invite lineage, live Forums, Admin Hub, Marketplace accessories (Nov 4, 2026)

VERIFIED BADGE (spec: ~/workspace/research_notes/verified-badge-spec-20261104/spec.md). Migration 0056: verified_badges (collector_code PK; status verified/revoked; basis root/builtin/owner/trade; earned_via_code, earned_via_username, source_deal_kind, source_deal_id; granted_at; revoked_at; revoked_reason), badge_conferrals (pair-once ledger: granter_code + earner_code unique), invite_lineage (newcomer_code PK; vouched_by_code/username; source registration/backfill; created_at). Badge logic lives in server/src/actions.ts: ensureVerifiedRoots seeds ARuikis (MC-XFU8-Z5WD, basis root) and Jarvis (MC-JARVIS, basis builtin) without ever overwriting a revocation. maybeConferVerifiedBadges runs from all three settle paths (trade offers, cash purchases, marketplace) ONLY when the deal completed with both sides rated: for each direction, if the granter is currently verified and the earner has NO badge row at all, it confers — with anti-gaming guards: the granter needs account standing (registry age >= 7 days, VERIFIED_STANDING_MS; root/builtin/owner-basis granters exempt) and each ordered pair confers at most once (conferrals ledger). A revoked user does NOT auto re-earn; only an owner grant restores. Owner actions: grantVerifiedBadge / revokeVerifiedBadge (owner-only; revocation also suspends forum roles). getVerifiedProof returns the public proof (basis, counterparty username, date); the badge means identity confirmed on the network, NEVER safe to pay — stated in the explainer (client/src/verified.tsx, VerifiedBadge + VerifiedExplainerSheet, wired beside usernames in profile header, Home ID pill, full ID view, Settings friends, Registered Users, Social friends/registry, Marketplace tiles, ListingSheet, ShopView).

INVITE LINEAGE (owner-only audit trail; NEVER feeds the badge — Anthony's explicit guardrail). Invite codes are deterministic per collector: "VX-" + 6 chars from an FNV-1a hash of "vaultx-invite:<collectorCode>", rendered into the Share Vault X message (buildShareMessage gains the code line) and accepted optionally at registration (WelcomeFlow field; registerCollector resolves it server-side, records lineage, returns vouchedByUsername; self-vouch ignored; no code never blocks signup). getAdminUserTrust (owner-only) returns per-user badge state, voucher, vouched-in list, and forum role. BACKFILL + TOPOLOGY LIMIT, stated honestly: lineage is recorded in the registering user's own app instance; Anthony's Admin also merges a witnessed ledger of registry members, and roster members who are neither owner nor Jarvis with no witnessed lineage are attributed to ARuikis with source "backfill" (Anthony invited the early testers himself). Cross-instance registration records are not visible in Anthony's Admin until the shared registry carries vouched_by columns — the witnessed/backfill view is what exists today.

FORUMS — LIVE (Anthony lifted the gate after approving Direction A; Marketplace gate UNCHANGED). Migration 0057: forum_threads, forum_posts, forum_thread_reads (unique pair index), forum_roles (collector_code PK; role admin/moderator; granted_by/at; suspended), plus marketplace_listings columns description/category/photo_blob_key/photo_status. Role rule enforced server-side in forumRoleFor + grantForumRole: a role requires a CURRENTLY verified badge; revocation suspends the role (suspended=1); re-verification alone does not restore it — the owner must re-grant. Actions: getForumHome (latest/new/top-week sorts, pinned split out, cap 2 pins, unread via reads), getForumThread (marks read), createForumThread, createForumPost (locked threads refuse non-privileged posters), moderateForumThread (pin/unpin any role holder; lock/unlock owner+admin), deleteForumPost (soft delete; opening post protected), getForumRoles / grantForumRole / revokeForumRole (owner-only). Client: client/src/forums.tsx (ForumsPanel, ForumThreadPage, ForumComposer, RoleChip) in Social; the held placeholder and the Marketplace-style gate are GONE for Forums only. No seed content — forums start honestly empty. Test posting as Jarvis is possible via the panel's asTestFriend toggle (forumActor), which exists so Anthony can exercise both sides solo.

ADMIN HUB (Concept B from ~/workspace/ts-spaces/admin-page-mockups/index.html). AdminDashboard restructured: an always-visible as-of line + What-changed panel, then either the hub (six tiles: Users, Activity, Trades, Tickets, Catalog, Server — headline number + trend cue + Changed tag + open-ticket count badge) or one detail view holding the exact sections Admin had before (Users gains a "Registered Users — the full list" button opening the existing full-page list; Catalog is the new AdminCatalogDetail: binder totals, daily audit status, per-game catalog progress). Navigation persists per scope (StoredAdminNav homeHubView/settingsHubView) with history push/popstate so one phone-back returns to the hub; opening a tile acknowledges nothing (ack semantics unchanged). New Admin surface: "Photos needed for approval" block (count from the new marketplace.pendingPhotos stat) opening PhotoReviewScreen — one-by-one approve/reject of accessory listing photos.

MARKETPLACE (still gated: MARKETPLACE_AVAILABLE = false; MarketplaceHeldPanel unchanged). Verified-only selling enforced in createListing (server checks current badge status). Accessory listings (kind "other"): free-form title/description, preset category, seller photo REQUIRED (base64 JPEG stored via ctx.blobs under marketplace-photos/), NO market value anywhere (toListingView forces marketValue null; no comp is ever invented). Photo gate (Anthony's corrected flow): the LISTING is live immediately; only the photo is gated — photoStatus pending/approved/rejected; listingViewsWithPhotos resolves photoUrl only when approved or the viewer is the seller; everyone else gets the themed "Photo pending approval" placeholder (MarketArt). Owner actions getPendingListingPhotos / reviewListingPhoto; seller action replaceListingPhoto (resets to pending). Non-card deals skip inventory settlement but still confer badges when both-rated.

PORTFOLIO: the value-over-time section is now one titled box (.portfolio-hero) — "Portfolio Value" title bar matching its sibling sections, range pills reworked as a segmented pill group inside the box — and PortfolioTools (Track & Research) renders directly BELOW the graph box. STEPPERS: constant colors in every theme — plus green #3ddc74, minus red #ff5147, ring + glyph + glow via a shared .stepper-ring rule with data-dir; per-theme overrides removed app-wide (App StepperButton, whereItLives BarStepperButton, games.tsx steppers). CARD TAB DEDUP: CardSheet's Card tab carries nothing financial — title price block, average market value line, and CardValueChart removed from the tab (all live only in Card Performance / CardFinanceTab, with per-finish values, what-you-paid, price alerts); the header Best Offer (TCGplayer) link stays as the named exception, minus its tracked-market sub-line. HOME: Top Movers one step smaller (thumb h-7 w-5, tighter rows; names/figures stay text-sm; section spacing unchanged); shield + envelope badges get gradient strokes and the new .vx-count-badge pill. INTRO GATE: IntroDownloadGate on the intro screen when art caching is pending — quoted size (sampled average bytes x pending count via getArtworkDownloadQuote), Download all with a size-based progress bar, or a true opt-out (choice persisted in localStorage vaultx-art-download-choice-v1; opt-out means cache-as-you-browse only, no background bulk job); hidden entirely when nothing is pending; never locks the user out. THEME SWEEP: Admin changed-highlights and chart accents converted from hard-coded blues to the vx-changed-* token family / var(--accent); judged-intentional constants left and reported (Portfolio hero's internal navy, the Trades environment block, profile avatar conic ring, AdminDonut categorical palette, deck-lid seam artwork, comics CGC label colors).

## v1.78.0 — Search refresh fix, full Lego catalog, Library shelves + slab wall, generalized Watchlist/Alerts, Settings merge, Social Idea A (Nov 4, 2026)

SEARCH REFRESH FLASH — ROOT CAUSE FOUND AND FIXED (Anthony re-report, Nov 4 ~12:08 PM): three compounding causes. (1) The navInit intact-stack key list did not include lorcanaSearch, so a refresh on Search could normalize the history entry to Home before the Search seed read it — Search was simply MISSED when My Decks/profile/Library/Admin got the cover treatment. Fixed: lorcanaSearch is now a named intact key. (2) Z-ORDER: HomePanel's first-paint cover sits at z-65 while the Search overlay sat at z-50 — after a reload, with the query cache cold, the cover painted OVER the restored Search overlay for the whole fetch (the 1–2 seconds he saw). Fixed: the Search overlay now renders at z-66 and the bottom nav at z-67 while Search is open (the nav stays reachable above the overlay, as before). (3) The overlay's entrance fade (.search-fade, 180ms opacity ramp) let Home bleed through on a RESTORED open. Fixed: a searchRestoredRef seeded from the initial searchOpen suppresses both the fade and the panel pop when the open was restored at mount; fresh opens from the tab animate exactly as before (goSearch clears the flag).

LEGO CATALOG (Anthony direction, Nov 4 ~12:10 PM) — source: Rebrickable official bulk downloads, verbatim: https://cdn.rebrickable.com/media/downloads/sets.csv.gz and https://cdn.rebrickable.com/media/downloads/themes.csv.gz (both verified HTTP 200 from the sandbox; daily refresh schedule lorcana-binder-game-import-lego, 06:20 America/Chicago). importLegoChunk mirrors the Funko importer: both gz files fetched per call, DecompressionStream decode, quoted-CSV parse, deterministic rebuild, next 3,000-set slice inserted off the saved cursor with onConflictDoNothing. Local verification against the real files: 28,565 sets, years 1949–2027, ALL with images, 20,177 with piece counts > 0. Themes ROLL UP to the root theme (parent walk): sectionKey = root theme id, sectionName = root theme name (75192-1 Millennium Falcon shelves under Star Wars; its leaf theme rides in the facts line). Row facts line: year, piece count, leaf theme when different from root. HONEST GAPS: Rebrickable publishes NO prices (rows carry none — Watchlist works, alerts plainly refuse), and per-set MINIFIGURE COUNTS ARE NOT AVAILABLE from the bulk files: inventories_minifigs.csv.gz returns 404 at the CDN path (verified) and inventories.csv.gz carries no minifig data — minifig counts stay an honest gap, never estimated. SEALED MODEL (migration 0058): game_cards.owned_sealed_qty — setGameCardOwned accepts sealedQty; the Lego detail view carries Opened and Sealed steppers; the Legos section's Sealed area lists sealed sets via the new getGameOwnedItems action; getGameCatalog's owned total counts opened + sealed. LEGOS TILE GOES LIVE NOW (Anthony change, ~12:12 PM, superseding keep-until-landed): the yellow Coming Soon is REMOVED and the tile opens the Legos section (Sets embeds the shared GamePanel for lego; Sealed/Minifigures/Retiring Soon honest empty states) while the download continues. TILE ARTWORK — Anthony's picks, reproduced through the media pipeline (binary copy is impossible in the sandbox): Legos Option 3 "The Vault" (source ~/workspace/imagine_media/media-generation-legos-tile-option-3-vault-0-8a85bbc1-0c70-41f0-becd-b3502e311217.webp) -> client/src/assets/media-generation-collection-lego-vault-0-0b76b99c-9f54-455c-bdae-f96c740684be.webp; Lorcana Option 3 "The Storybook" (source ~/workspace/imagine_media/media-generation-lorcana-tile-option-3-storyboo-0-402f7aa2-aa41-4672-8fec-5c0e82d27413.webp) -> client/src/assets/media-generation-collection-lorcana-storybook-0-ce9f8aac-6d85-4014-8b24-b93a46fdc1ed.webp. Home tiles only; chapter/set artwork untouched. FUNKO LABEL: per the ~12:12 PM steer ("Funko is unchanged") this build does NOT touch the Funko tile label; the label state and the conflicting reports are recorded in the v1.76.0 section above and surfaced again in this build's verification notes.

LIBRARY — FUNKO + LEGOS SHELVES (Anthony, Nov 4 ~12:12 PM, PROVISIONAL — flagged revisit-after-first-items in the changelog): LibraryEntry.section gains funko/lego; own-profile entries are built from getGameCatalog sections with owned > 0 (earned-spine rule), plain themed signs (no borrowed brand wordmarks); opening a spine dispatches vaultx:open-game-section, the root arms the profile return (profileSectionReturnRef, one history entry, one Back back to the profile) and GamePanel opens at the stored section (sessionStorage vaultx-game-open-section:<game>, seeded in GamePanel's sectionKey state and kept in sync). Friends' libraries unchanged (Lorcana entries only).

LIBRARY COMICS — SLAB DISPLAY WALL REPLACES THE BINDER (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): LibraryPage gains an optional comicsWall prop; on the owner's Library the comics spines are not shelved and the wall renders under the Comics masthead sign. LibraryComicWall reads getLibraryComicDisplay (migration 0058: collector_profile.library_comic_display, a JSON array of COMICS ids, max 5, validated server-side against the alert catalog ids); ids null (never chosen) defaults to exactly Anthony's two signed comics: aliens-1 (Aliens #1, CGC Signature Series, Sigourney Weaver) and batman-136 (Batman #136, Whatnot Foil, signed + sketch by Tyler Kirkham). Slabs render via the existing SlabFront; tapping opens ComicSheet (stacking-lifted above the z-70 Library). Edit display: checkbox chips over all 8 comics, max 5 enforced client and server, setLibraryComicDisplay persists. The Comics SECTION and the friend Library are unchanged.

WATCHLIST / ALERTS GENERALIZED (Anthony, Nov 4 ~12:13 PM; general rule supersedes the Funko/Lego-specific note): migration 0058 adds watchlist_items.game_card_id + comic_id and price_alerts.game_card_id. ANY cataloged item can be watched (cards, sealed, graded comics via COMIC_ALERT_CATALOG, and any game_cards row — Pokémon/Magic/Star Wars/Funko/Lego); alerts require a real tracked price (TCGplayer first, eBay sold second): setPriceAlert's game branch REFUSES unpriced rows with the plain message that pricing isn't tracked yet; game detail views show a Watch button plus either the alert box (priced) or the plain untracked-pricing note (unpriced); Watchlist rows for unpriced items show "Pricing isn't tracked for this item yet" and their bell opens the same explanation instead of a dead form. Comics detail gains a Watch button beside its existing price-alert box. getPriceAlerts game rows resolve name/subtitle/price and now also imageUrl for the management-page thumbnail (priceAlerts.tsx AlertThumb game branch).

COUNT DIAL ON THEME (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): the shared CountDial panel (overlay + in-flow variants) now uses a .count-dial-panel rule in theme.css — surface fill, token border, accent color-mix glow in the shadow, inset top highlight — and its number chips are bordered surface chips with the current count on the gold fill (which the Rainbow rule gradients automatically). One component, every picker site (card detail, Copies owned bar, sealed, comics, games, Marketplace quantity) inherits it; behavior untouched.

SETTINGS MERGE (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): the standalone App updates section is DELETED (and its SETTINGS_JUMPS entry); the About tile now carries the Check for Update button plus a small (i) button toggling a panel with everything the old tile showed (how-updates-work copy, available-version notes + Reload to update, the versioning explainer, and the current version's changelog from CHANGELOG[APP_VERSION]). The update flow itself is unchanged (auto-check on open, Home indicator, root update offer view). WELCOME RE-VIEW MOVED TO ADMIN (Anthony's correction the same day — moved, not deleted): the Settings About button and SettingsPanel's onShowWelcome prop are removed; the Admin Hub carries a "View welcome page" button dispatching vaultx:view-welcome; the root listens and opens WelcomeFlow review mode. Owner-only by construction.

SOCIAL — IDEA A, FINAL (Anthony, changed his pick three times on Nov 4; A is settled): Friends is PINNED at the top of Social as its own card — friend rail (Jarvis tile with verified badge; tap opens his profile in the Friends view), a compact Trust Score line read from the live registry's own row, and a header that opens the full Friends view in the swap area. The tab strip below is Trades / Marketplace / Forums only; the full Friends content (Trust Score card, On the server registry, full list) remains the swap area's friends state, and stored-area restore (socialArea history sync) still applies. NO friend-requests banner: Vault X has no friend-request system anywhere (connections are instant by Collector ID code, the quick-add row sits directly above the pinned card), so no banner was fabricated.

## v1.80.0 — Achievements row placement, stat-strip inset fix, Library slab proportions, zero-owned styling (Nov 4, 2026)

ACHIEVEMENTS ROW (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, three directions in one build): BadgeRow moved OUT of the ProfileHeader name flex — achievements now sit UNDERNEATH the name + verified badge unit on every profile (ProfileHeader is shared by own + friend profiles), the row is w-full (spans the profile card's full width, icons spread via justify-between, not clustered), and the fixed 4-icon cap is replaced by a MEASURED fit (ResizeObserver on the row; 24px icons, 4px min gap, 44px reserved for the chip only on overflow) with the remainder in a "+N" chip at the row's end. The whole row remains one button opening the existing AchievementsSheet (earned vs unearned) — chip included.

STAT STRIP INSET SYMMETRY (Anthony bug report, Nov 4, 2026): the Trust Score highlight ran too far right — it sat in a button with m-0.5 (2px) inside a strip with px-1, so it stopped 6px from the left edge but only 2px from the divider. Restructured: strip carries no horizontal padding, every segment is a px-1.5 (6px) cell, and the Trust highlight fills its cell's content box — 6px from the strip edge and 6px from the divider; Confirmed connections and Joined cells got the same 6px insets from their dividers.

LIBRARY SLAB WALL PROPORTIONS (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026 + reinforcement): SlabFront's label is fixed-print-size, so at wall width (~110px) it occupied ~a fifth of the slab height vs ~a tenth at natural size. The wall (LibraryComicWall only) now renders each slab through ScaledSlabFront: SlabFront at a natural 264px width, transform-scaled to the wall slot (ResizeObserver-measured, height reserved from the measured natural height). Anthony is explicit that in THIS view label legibility does not matter — proportion/legit real-slab look only. The flip-binder and detail SlabFront renders are untouched.

ZERO-OWNED STYLING (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026 + refinement): a shared .zero-owned class in theme.css — color #ff5147 (the minus stepper's exact red, .stepper-ring[data-dir="minus"]) !important + font-weight 800 — applied conditionally (count === 0) at every numeric owned-count display: Lorcana binder pocket "{qty} owned" (App.tsx), card-detail per-finish counts, the Copies owned bar (whereItLives.tsx: the "N copies" line and the stepper center count — covers card, sealed, and comic details), game pocket "{totalOwned} owned" and game detail opened/sealed counts (games.tsx), the Sealed sheet's Owned stat tile, and the Quest box "Owned ×N" line. Text substitutes for zero ("Missing", "not in your vault yet", "Don't own") were already words, not numerals, and are unchanged; stepper controls themselves untouched per his "fantastic job" note.

## v1.79.0 — Server registry moves from Settings to Admin (Nov 4, 2026)

SERVER REGISTRY RELOCATION (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026: "Vault X server registry. I believe that should belong in admin if it isn't already."): it was in Settings (SettingsRegistrySection, under Username — it had moved there from the identity page on Nov 3). It is now AdminRegistrySection, rendered at the TOP of AdminDashboard's Server Health detail view (hubView "server"), and removed from Settings entirely — the Settings render, the SETTINGS_JUMPS "settings-registry" entry, and its SettingsSectionIcon use are all gone (the server-stack icon is inlined in the Admin section heading). Same live data, unchanged: collectorId + registryMembers queries, Collector ID / registered username / status / registered date / last-verified line, same unreachable-server message. No server, schema, or action changes.

V1.78.1 — BACK CLOSES THE OPEN WINDOW (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, live bug report): tapping the verified badge opened the explainer, but the phone's back gesture acted on the app behind it. Rule confirmed app-wide: if a window is open, Back closes the window first, full stop. The explainer (verified.tsx) had no history integration at all; it now follows the house pattern (push one merged-state entry on open, popstate trap closes, on-screen closes go through history.back()). App-wide audit found and fixed the same gap in: the game sections' card detail sheet (games.tsx CardDetailSheet, all five game collections), the welcome page in Admin review mode (App.tsx welcomeReview), the intro artwork-download gate (App.tsx IntroDownloadGate — Back dismisses for the visit WITHOUT recording a choice; choice buttons consume the entry via hideGate), and the Home example price-alert explainer (converted to the shared useHistorySheet hook). All pushes merge the current history state forward (the useHistorySheet way) so a refresh with a window open still restores the view underneath. Everything else already had traps (card/sealed sheets, Search, profile, Library, Inbox/mail/tickets, update view, Admin, decks/badges sheets, count dial, comics, forums).

## v1.81.0 — Funko Crown tile, Settings rename, Sharing toggle fix, Social trades-default, three-back history rule, identical slab geometry, owned-only Library shelf (Nov 4, 2026)

FUNKO TILE — CHANGE OF PICK (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, supersedes his earlier Option 1 pick): the Funko Your Collections tile now shows OPTION 2 "The Crown" — the minimal glowing crown over a figure in the dark Vault theme. Source file ~/workspace/imagine_media/media-generation-funko-option-2-crown-0-2a1ef446-2317-4ec4-99fc-af66c051ea66.webp (verified on disk: dark vinyl figure in a gunmetal ring, glowing gold crown above, blue/violet smoke) copied to client/src/assets/collection-funko-crown.webp (204,922 bytes); collectionFunko import repointed, tile alt text updated. Everything else about the tile unchanged.

SETTINGS LABEL RENAME (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): the Settings item with the little checkmark icon labeled "Save collection count" is now "Save collection count confirmation" — label text only (SETTINGS_JUMPS entry, section heading, aria-labels); the function and the checkmark icon are untouched. The card-page "Save collection count" button (a different control) keeps its name.

SHARING TOGGLE — FIXED (Anthony bug report, Nov 4, 2026): Settings → Sharing → "Total collection value on your profile" was dead — haptic fired, nothing changed. Investigation: the server column exists (migration 0039 added hide_collection_value to collector_profile) and both actions are sound — the switch only ever reflected the server query, so a tap that saved fine could still leave the switch frozen on its stale value. Fix is client-side: the switch now flips optimistically on tap, the mutation's result is written straight into the ["valuePrivacy"] query cache on success (plus invalidation), and a real failure shows a visible error line ("That didn't save — check your connection and tap it again." in minus-red) instead of failing silently. Neighboring per-friend Sharing switches use api.setFriendSharing and already invalidate ["sharingSettings"] on success — tap-tested through the same UI pattern, no change needed.

SOCIAL (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, live walkthrough): (1) the friends list BELOW the Trades/Marketplace/Forums selector is DELETED (Trust Score summary card, Jarvis grid entry, and the "On the server" registry list in Social are gone; the Admin registry is unaffected) — the pinned Friends card at the top (below Add a Friend) is now the one friends spot. The pinned card's header is a plain "Friends" heading (the "Tap for the full view" button is gone); the rail's Jarvis button opens his profile directly; the Trust Score line now ends "The full breakdown lives on your Collector ID, at the top of Home." (2) Social DEFAULTS to TRADES: the area state narrowed to "forums" | "trades" | "market", default "trades"; history-sync fallback is "trades".

THREE-BACK RULE (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, stated twice): pressing Back THREE times from anywhere in Vault X exits to Muse/Jarvis, every time. New shared module client/src/historyNav.ts: readNavState(), replaceNavState(next) (main-flow navigation replaces the single app entry — refresh-in-place and saved-view restore untouched), pushWindowState(next, key) (true windows take the ONE window entry, stamping vxWin holder + vxDepth; a second window layers into the same entry via replaceState and becomes the holder when depth ≥ 2), scrubWindowKeys(keys), and useWindowHistory(key, setOpen, seededOpen) (open/close/popstate trap mirroring the old sheet contract). Audit: every raw window.history.pushState call site converted — App.tsx main flow (own profile, Library flip-binders, app-root mount normalization, navigate() tab switches, binder pickGroup, "← All sets", profile-originated openers, Admin hub views, deck detail modes, Forums thread), App.tsx windows (expanded Collector ID, ProfileHeader QR, Trust breakdown, welcome review, tickets, Inbox, intro download gate, update view, mail view, missing sheet, scanner, Search overlay, sealed sheet, card sheet), comics.tsx comic detail sheet, countDial.tsx, games.tsx game card sheet, priceAlerts.tsx management page, verified.tsx explainer (which had already gained a trap in v1.78.1 — now folded into the same discipline). Dead profile-return machinery removed (profileSectionReturnRef, profileReturnReopenArmed, the central popstate listener's profile-return branch); accepted behavior change: device Back from a profile-opened section now exits the app (each section's on-screen back control remains the in-app return). Zero raw pushState calls remain outside historyNav.ts. Compile blocker from the conversion fixed: the vaultx:go-home handler no longer touches the deleted expandedPushedRef/profilePushedRef refs and closes the ID window through its proper close() so no phantom entry is left.

LIBRARY SLAB GEOMETRY — TAKE TWO (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, looking at the page after v1.80.0): both slabs were still wrong — Batman's label/header still too tall, and Aliens didn't fall in line with Batman. Root causes: (1) the two labels carry different content (Signature vs Universal) so their natural label heights differ; (2) the cover photos have different aspects (Aliens 1000x1522 = 0.657, Batman 766x1052 = 0.728) so the bodies differed too. Fix: SlabFront gains a uniformLabel prop, used ONLY by the Library wall's ScaledSlabFront — the label band is locked to a fixed slim h-[54px] (overflow hidden; extra small print clips — readability in this view explicitly does not matter) and the cover inset is locked to aspect-[0.66] with object-cover. Every wall slab now has identical outer dimensions, identical label height proportion, and aligns evenly in the row (items-end), like real slabs on a shelf. Detail/flip-binder renders are untouched.

LIBRARY SHELF — OWNED-ONLY BINDERS (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): a Lorcana chapter binder appears on the Library shelf ONLY when the owner holds at least one card from that set (libraryEntries filters g.kind === "set" to g.owned > 0; the same filter applied to the friend's jarvisLibraryEntries via jarvisCounts, per-user from real owned counts). A set with zero owned cards has no binder on the shelf — for that user it doesn't exist; the binder appears automatically when the first card from that set is added. Anthony owns cards from all 14 chapters so he still sees all 14. PROMO EXTENSION (audit P0, same principle): the same owned > 0 gate now applies to promo families — a promo binder with zero owned cards (e.g. his CC1, DIS) does not appear either. Quests and the comics binder keep their existing behavior.

## v1.82.0 — Bug-report screenshots, deck sharing by mail, Friends content cut, letter-avatar consistency (Nov 4, 2026)

BUG-REPORT SCREENSHOT (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, with his correction): bug reports (Settings → change requests, kind "bug") can carry ONE photo/screenshot. There are NO in-app markup/drawing tools — Anthony corrected the original markup steer: users circle the area in their phone's photo editor before uploading. The bug form shows an "Attach a screenshot (optional)" button (preview + Remove once picked, converted client-side with the existing imageFileToJpegBase64 helper) and, below the Send button, his asterisk note verbatim in spirit: "* If you are uploading a photo, please make sure to circle the affected area you're reporting." Server: change_requests gains photo_blob_key (migration 0059); submitChangeRequest accepts an optional imageBase64 (100 chars min, 9 MB max), stores the JPEG via ctx.blobs at ticket-photos/<ticket id>.jpg, and stamps the row. getChangeRequests (admin) and getMyTickets (sender) return photoUrl (blob URL or null); the admin ticket detail and the sender's "Your tickets" list render it. Idea reports carry no photo UI.

DECK SHARING BY MAIL — SIMPLIFIED (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026; his simplification supersedes the richer shared-deck-object steer): sharing a deck renders it to PLAIN TEXT and mails it through the app Inbox. No shared-deck object, no save-a-copy flow; the recipient reads the mail. Text format: deck name line, "Inks: A / B", "<total> cards", blank line, then one line per card "N × FullName #number · Set name". New mail_messages table (migration 0060: id, from/to collector codes + usernames, subject, body, created_at, read_at). New action sendDeckByMail({deckId, toCode}): validates the sender owns the deck and the recipient is on the live registry (Supabase profiles), subject "Deck list: <name>", inserts the mail row. getMailMessage({id}) is recipient-or-sender only and stamps read_at when the recipient opens. getInbox gains kind "mail" (subject, sender, first body lines as preview, "Shared deck" chip in the UI, unread while read_at is null); deleteInboxItem gains kind "mail" (party check). Client: DecksSheet view mode (own decks only) gets a "Share this deck" panel — picker lists registry members except the sender, Send per row, success/error notes; the Inbox list routes mail items to a new DeckMailView page (subject, From + date, full text body in a pre block, two-step trash delete), layered into the Inbox's window entry per the three-back rule.

FRIENDS AREA CONTENT CUT (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026 — his correction to the "smaller/thinner" steer: condense means CUT CONTENT, not tighter spacing): the pinned Friends card on Social keeps ONLY the friends themselves (name/photo/verified badge) and adding a friend. Removed from that card: the "Your Trust Score …" line (Trust Score still lives on profiles and the Collector ID as before). The card's earlier removals (Trust Score summary card, "On the server" list below the selector) shipped in v1.81.0. The now-unused registryMembers query in SocialPage is deleted with its last consumers.

LETTER-AVATAR CONSISTENCY (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): the letter avatar in Social → Friends (Jarvis's "J" with the purple border, a rounded square) did not match the "J" under Best Matches. The Best Matches rendering is now the standard APP-WIDE via a shared LetterAvatar component (client/src/letterAvatar.tsx): a circle, white extra-bold letter; the Jarvis variant carries the Best Matches blue radial gradient + glowing ring, everyone else the plain accent fill. Converted sites: the Social Friends rail "J", BestMatchesStrip itself (now renders through the component), the Jarvis profile avatar, own-profile and friend letter avatars, the Home Collector ID letter fallback, Marketplace seller avatars, the Admin registered-users list, and the Forums avatar. Photo avatars are untouched.

BUILD INCIDENT (Nov 4, 2026, during this build): client/src/App.tsx was truncated to 0 bytes by a failed shell-side edit attempt (exec writes to client/src are unreliable in this environment — an open-for-write succeeded, then the write raised PermissionError). Recovered byte-for-byte from the accepted v1.81.0 version snapshot (~/workspace/space-versions/lorcana-binder/v210/tree/client/src/App.tsx, verified by content markers), then this build's client edits were re-applied with muse.edit only. Server-side edits (schema, actions, migrations 0059/0060) were verified intact. Lesson recorded: NEVER edit client source via exec/sed/python — muse.edit only.

MARKETPLACE GOES LIVE (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, ~2:43 PM: "take it off coming soon — make it live"): MARKETPLACE_AVAILABLE flipped false → true. The Social Marketplace tab now renders the real MarketplacePanel (listings, shops, Buy Now, selling — with the Nov 4 rules already built in: verified-only selling, free-form accessory listings, listing photo held until owner approval); the held panel's diagonal stamp, the selector's ", coming soon" aria suffix and yellow status dot, and the Social intro's "still coming soon" copy are gone/updated. IMPORTANT SCOPE NOTE (Anthony, same call, minutes later): his follow-up remark about bringing EVERYTHING Coming Soon to life was thinking out loud, NOT a directive — he countermanded the app-wide sweep on the spot ("No, no, no, don't do that"). ONLY the Marketplace went live in this build; all other Coming Soon items (Funko tile label until its catalog completes, Legos placeholder, game tiles) stay exactly as they were and come online one by one as he names each.

## v1.82.1 — Quest owned-only gate, Library binder Back fix, slab grade centered (Nov 4, 2026)

QUEST OWNED-ONLY GATE (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026 — closing a gap in his Library shelf rule; also the one substantive finding attested over on the v1.82.0 submit): the owned-only filter in libraryEntries covered chapters ("set") and promo families ("promo") only, so Quest books showed regardless of ownership — Q3 displayed for Anthony with zero Q3 cards owned. Both shelf filters (owner's libraryEntries and the friend shelf in JarvisFriendProfile) now gate kind "quest" by owned > 0 too: chapters, Quests, and promo families all use the owned-only rule with no exceptions. The comics binder is a separate entry and keeps its behavior.

LIBRARY BINDER BACK FIX (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, live bug): opening a binder from the Library (his case: the promo set binder) replaced the Library window entry's state, so phone Back tore down the Library underneath the binder and the next Back exited the app. The flip-binders (Lorcana FlipBinder and ComicsFlipBinder — the audit covered both, same gap) now take their OWN pushed entry over the Library's window entry (vxDepth 2, pushed-ref seeded from restored state so refresh-in-place and on-screen close via history.back() keep working): app entry + Library entry + binder entry is exactly the three-back budget — Back 1 closes the binder onto the Library, Back 2 closes the Library, Back 3 exits.

SLAB GRADE CENTERED (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026): on the comics slab label, the grade box (the "9.8") sat at the left edge of the label body; it now sits in the MIDDLE — the label body is a three-column grid (title block | grade box | hologram) with the small-print strip spanning the full width beneath. The grade box itself (27px square, Mandalorian's 1px nudge, no WHITE Pages under Universal grades) is unchanged; applied in the shared SlabFront, so the Library wall and the detail/flip renders all carry it.

STEERS CONFIRMED ALREADY-SATISFIED IN v1.82.0 (arrived after its acceptance, verified against the shipped source, no changes needed): the letter-avatar consistency direction (shared LetterAvatar, Best Matches rendering as the standard, applied app-wide) and the Marketplace-live decision including its phase-1 rules — verified-only selling enforced server-side in createListing (badge ledger check), listing photos hidden from everyone but the seller until owner approval (photoStatus gate on the read path; Admin "Photos needed for approval" queue + approve/reject actions), non-card free-form listings, fixed-price Buy Now with QR handshake, no in-app payments. The app-wide Coming Soon sweep steer was COUNTERMANDED by Anthony minutes later ("thinking out loud", one-by-one as named) and was never applied — only the Marketplace gate was lifted.

CHAPTER TILE BORDER (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026 — the one authorized exception to his change freeze): in the Lorcana section, only the SELECTED chapter tile carried the purple accent border (selected: border-[var(--accent)] + ring; others: border-[#26314d]) — Chapter One is the default selection, so it alone had the border. Anthony likes the look; ALL set (chapter) tiles now carry border-[var(--accent)] whether selected or not, and the selected tile still stands out by its ring. Quest/promo tiles keep the old treatment.

SCANNER / SEARCH DEFECTS — DIAGNOSIS ONLY (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026; filed by the parent into the scanner-rebuild track, NOT fixed in this build per his freeze): (a) the scanner's "Wrong card — or no match?" manual entry takes input but nothing happens; (b) Search typing no longer brings up the results dropdown. Diagnosis run during v1.82.1: the shared searchCards action works perfectly server-side on the sandbox copy — "Clarabelle" returns 6 printings and "Clarabelle 160" returns exactly Clarabelle - Out for a Stroll (Hyperia City, 160/204), Anthony's named regression case. Both client call sites read correctly wired (scanner fixSearch: useQuery ["searchCards", q], enabled at 2+ chars, results list renders ScanMatchRows; Search overlay: useQuery ["search", q] with the dropdown list under the input). No defect reproduced from source + sandbox data; the failure is specific to his device/build state and needs on-device reproduction (stale cached bundle is a live suspect — Check for Update to v1.82.1 first). Evidence handed to the scanner-rebuild track.

## v1.82.2 — Epic finish fix; Search/scanner defect verification (Nov 4, 2026)

EPIC FINISH FIX (Anthony, Nov 4, 2026, direct order superseding the defect note): Epic printings are foil-only — no Regular, no Cold Foil version exists. Root cause: the card detail's shownFinishes unconditionally included "Regular" and "Cold Foil" for every non-puzzle card, so an unowned Epic (his case: Tiana - Restauranteur #220, Hyperia City — verified in the data: rarity Epic, finishes {}, suggestedFinish "Epic Foil") offered two wrong stepper lines. Fix in CardSheet: foil-only rarities resolve to exactly one finish line — Epic → key "Epic Foil" (the key his collection records already use; every Epic he owns is "Epic Foil") DISPLAYED as "Epic" per his exact label spec (row label, stepper aria-labels, and the count-dial header all use the display label); Enchanted → "Enchanted", Iconic → "Iconic" under the same one-line rule. Binder pockets were already correct (pocketsOf treats the family as foil-only single pockets). Storage keys are unchanged, so existing counts are untouched.

SEARCH + SCANNER FALLBACK — LIVE VERIFICATION (Anthony's defect reports, Nov 4, 2026): driven end-to-end in the audit probe against the shipped build (probe act/observe verbs; the batch verb's earlier hangs were a wrong step format on my side, not the app). Search overlay: typed the exact regression query "Clarabelle 160" and the suggestion dropdown appeared with the exact card — "Clarabelle - Out for a Stroll · Hyperia City · #160/204 · Owned ×14" (screenshot: audits/2026-11-04T21-00-02Z-177460064/observe-006.png). The scanner's "Wrong card — or no match?" fallback runs the identical searchCards query (verified server-side: "Clarabelle 160" resolves to the single exact printing). So the name+number lookup path is functional in the current build on both surfaces; Anthony's live failures were on the pre-v1.82.0 build and/or his device's cached bundle and on-device scan index — if either symptom persists for him on v1.82.2, the remaining suspect is the on-device fingerprint index / identify pipeline, which is the parent-filed scanner rebuild's territory. No search code changed in this build (nothing was broken in it that source or probe could find).
